系数证书方面，已得到若干**指定形式的排除推导**：源端三项约束、移动下标的源端流量、远端根部的前缀约束，以及最后一行的时钟矩约束。这些约束排除了具体的共同列和 PSD 余项选择，包括全部兼容的 skew 补全。投影 $H_4$ 路线中，若干起点约束和拼接权重的分配也被完整二次型中的负方向排除。它们没有排除一般证书锥，也没有给出真正违反目标不等式的合法函数。[一般终端接口及两种列的障碍](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/shared_evidence/8bcf300ff83e2288.md)

**五、Gray 与有限域路线的重要更正**

这一方向用 binary Gray code 和 Walsh characters 构造高维顶点，再要求这些顶点成为实际梯度。已经形成一个有用的候选接口：对固定、线性无关的顶点 $v_i$，把全部合法初始化转写为势函数 $F_i=\langle z_0,v_i\rangle$ 的全对差分约束，并保留到完整极锥最小点集合的真实距离。其 perspective 表达带有一个总是可行的零点；若原轨迹不可实现，必须明确这个空集边界，不能从优化表达中推断实际轨迹存在。

但是旧的径向衰减版本包含一个明确的计数错误。若前两级径向比记为 $x,y$，实际递推应为

$$
\sigma_k=x^{\lceil k/2\rceil}y^{\lfloor(k+2)/4\rfloor}.
$$

旧稿误用了 $\lfloor(k+1)/4\rfloor$，遗漏了相应的 level-one step。例如 $k=4q+2$ 的系数应为 $xy\theta^q$，不是 $x\theta^q$。该错误还进入了旧稿的有限生成多项式。

因此，旧稿所称覆盖全部合法初始化的 $343/(333D_m)$ 上界，现在应标为**待修复、待重新审阅的候选**。不能继续沿用“这一固定路线已经可靠排除”的表述。新报告明确没有重新核查旧上界；本次暂停整理也没有修复证明或重跑相关程序。错误公式本身已被指出，但尚不能据此断言旧上界的最终结论一定为假。

改为另一径向衰减参数后，最新草稿给出两个较窄结论：一个显式势函数在原始远距离约束处失败；同一两顶点 relaxation 则允许高于 $69/(64D_m)>17/(16D_m)$ 的收益。这只说明那一对子问题无法排除新的构造，**没有构造出满足全部原始约束的真实高维轨迹**。[更正与新径向版本报告](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/shared_evidence/a37b81ea81b53910.md)

**六、数值工作实际说明了什么**

最近的计算都有单独预算和校准。以下仅列会影响下一步判断的结果。

在同一 enclosing $m=1536$ 的有限 $H_3,H_4$ 上，以 $q=17/[16(1+2\sum h)]$ 搜索完整原始 dual，两种模式——自由终端参数 $\pi$ 和固定 $\pi=\sqrt q$——都返回了数值解。因而没有观察到这两类的一般可行性分离。H4 自由模式返回的 $\pi\approx-7.02\times10^{-12}$ 及其负乘子原样保留，没有被改写成精确零；其他模式也保留了小负特征值。这些不是精确证书。[有限 dual 诊断](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/terminal_mass_interior_discovery_20261003/HANDOFF.md)

最新一批直接最大化投影端点不等式的违反量

$$
V=U_1(T_hx_0)-U_{1+2\sum h}(x_0),\qquad \|x_0\|=1,
$$

纳入全部原始投影法向约束，得到：

| 有限算例 | 返回的 $V$ | 求解状态 |
|---|---:|---|
| $H_3$ | $1.0874\times10^{-9}$ | optimal |
| $H_4$ | $2.4904\times10^{-9}$ | optimal_inaccurate |

两项已知校准均通过，但上述生产值都接近零。H4 的最小原始 Gram 特征值为 $-1.5756\times10^{-10}$，最大原始法向残差为 $3.7285\times10^{-10}$，dual stationarity 缺陷约为 $1.8965\times10^{-8}$。因此这批结果没有给出可信的精确反例，也没有证明 $H_4$ 不等式。这两个生产算例的原始 Gram 都含小负特征值，坐标重建被跳过，没有通过裁剪特征值制造“实际轨迹”。[H3 原始摘要](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/projection_u_primal_discovery_20261003/03_H3/summary.json)；[H4 原始摘要](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/projection_u_primal_discovery_20261003/04_H4/summary.json)

此前 Gray 的几个有限字典算例只产生了带正残差的数值势函数；优化公共平移后也没有提供所需的真实下界见证。有限字典随维数改变，不能外推为完整 $2^m$ 维构造的合法性或渐近结论。

**七、恢复研究时值得先做的调整**

我的判断是，最值得保留的主线是完整投影模型及其全原始约束；继续仅在已经被排除的低秩证书形式上改几个系数，收益已经降低。下一步应先做来源修复和方向选择，再决定是否恢复调用。

1. 先处理 Gray 计数错误：定位受影响的公式、证明和数值构造，重新确定旧上界还能保留多少。不要把旧候选的结论直接带入新任务。
2. 若主攻反例方向，围绕真正需要的 $17/(16D_m)$ 全函数类上界重新选择证书结构。all-$d$ 的精确传播是可选工具，不是原目标的必需前置结论。
3. 若主攻下界构造，新的高维方案必须先满足全部原始插值或投影约束，再核对真实最近最小点距离。两顶点 relaxation 和数值势函数只能提供线索。
4. 双截断标量族目前剩下的具体类是至少三次穿越、较小原始半径、截断前终点。它可以作为独立子问题继续，但应明确它与全维主目标之间还缺桥梁。

**八、暂停状态与接续入口**

这项工作确实使用了 Danus：由其管理项目、共享结果、研究轮次和核查接收记录；研究后端使用 Codex，并辅以少量有明确额度的直接 Codex 调用。最新模型选择是 gpt-6-astra / ultra。

Goal 已标记为 PAUSED。四个 Danus 研究员均已无存活进程，最后一个未完成调用被终止；直接数值诊断已关闭，文档代理也已中断。已完成原始输出仍在，本次未启动新研究或正式核查。恢复须等你明确提出；不会因为存在旧任务文件或未用完的额度而自动重启。

最有用的接续入口是本总结、[原始完整问题](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/PROBLEM.md) 和 [暂停接续索引](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/PAUSED_HANDOFF_20261003.md)。索引另行保存精确文件、证据状态、未读报告和被中止的任务，便于下一次接续，而不把操作记录混入上述数学结论。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001634 -->
# 梯度下降精确常数问题阶段性总结

整理日期：2026 年 10 月 8 日。研究已按你的要求暂停，原始目标保持未解决。

目前最可靠的收获是：把原问题归约到有限几何模型，建立了完整终端证书接口，并核查接收了一个七步基块的精确最坏值。还没有得到原始渐近 minimax 等式，也没有得到足以否定它的全函数类上界。近期大量工作排除了具体证书形式、刻画了若干低维函数族；这些结果有助于选择下一步，但不能据此说已经接近完成主目标。

本文直接整理已经保存的材料，没有启动独立报告写作模型、重跑算例或进行新一轮证明核查。“已接收”指 Danus 核查记录已接收该明确陈述，不表示已经完成 Lean 等证明助手的形式化。尚未接收的推导、数值结果和存在错误的草稿分别标明。相对于 10 月 3 日的总结，本次新增内容主要是 Gray 计数修订稿、全初始化优化的推导与校准停止记录；没有新增已接收的数学结论。

**一、原始问题和目前选择的突破方向**

对任意预先给定的非负步长序列 $h=(h_0,\ldots,h_{n-1})$，考虑

$$
x_{t+1}=x_t-\frac{h_t}{L}\nabla f(x_t),\qquad
\mathcal W_n(h)=\sup\frac{2(f(x_n)-f_\star)}{L\|x_0-x_\star\|^2}.
$$

上确界覆盖所有有限维、具有最小点的 convex $L$-smooth 函数，以及所有合法起点和所选最小点。记

$$
\mathcal W_n^\star=\inf_{h\ge0}\mathcal W_n(h),\qquad
U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}=\frac1{B_n}.
$$

这里 $B_n=1+2S_\bullet(n)$ 是 Zhang–Jiang 的原始 dominant profile。我们要判断

$$
\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{\mathcal W_n^\star}{U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}}=1.
$$

肯定方向要求对所有充分大的整数 $n$ 和所有非负 $h$，统一证明 $\mathcal W_n(h)\ge(1-o(1))/B_n$。否定方向只需构造无界长度子序列及相应步长，并对**全部原始函数类**证明一个固定比例的改进。只匹配 Silver exponent、只处理少数长度或一个 adversary family，都不足以完成任务。全文保持 $\mathcal W=2\mathcal R$ 的归一化。

基准可等价写为 $C_0=B_0=1$，以及

$$
\begin{aligned}
\Omega(x,y)&=\frac{x+y+\sqrt{x^2+6xy+y^2}}2,\\
G(x,y)&=2x+\frac y2+\frac{\sqrt{y^2+8xy}}2,\\
C_n&=\max_{0\le k<n}\Omega(C_k,C_{n-k-1}),\\
B_n&=\max_{0\le k<n}G(C_k,B_{n-k-1}).
\end{aligned}
$$

这些最大值保留所有整数切分，不能用仅有平衡切分的序列替代。

**二、已经核查接收的主要数学成果**

| 成果 | 已建立的内容 | 对主目标的作用及边界 |
|---|---|---|
| Projection simplex 表示 | 每个固定有限步长序列的完整最坏值，可在下面定义的 origin-vertex simplex 函数类中取相同上确界 | 将任意光滑凸函数搜索转成有限几何问题；没有给出所需速率 |
| Affine-height Moreau envelope 表示 | 另一类至多 $n+2$ 个顶点的多面体模型，也保留完整有限序列上确界 | 提供了不同的具体构造接口；其梯度与前一模型不同 |
| Unqueried-terminal reduction | 完整性能估计可删去终点的 incoming rows，并选取相应紧的全空间证书 | 保留全部前缀约束；不允许随意删除远距离前缀行 |
| 精确基准比较 | $C_n\le(n+1)^\rho$、$B_n\le\frac{12}{5}(n+1)^\rho$，其中 $\rho=\log_2(1+\sqrt2)$；并得到下文候选步长族的比较桥梁 | 反例方向的基准算术已经落实；全函数类上界仍缺 |
| 实际七步基块 | 下文 $H_3^{(m)}$ 对全部原始函数类满足精确 Huber 值，并具有 all-body、all-$d$ 的投影不等式 | 是可靠基块；尚未推广到完整 $H_m^{(m)}$ |

Projection simplex 模型是

$$
F_D(x)=\max_{g\in D}\left\{\langle x,g\rangle-\tfrac12\|g\|^2\right\},
\qquad D=\operatorname{conv}\{0,w_1,\ldots,w_k\},
$$

其中 $w_1,\ldots,w_k$ 线性无关，$1\le k\le p+1$，$p$ 是序列中的正步数。它满足

$$
\nabla F_D=P_D,\qquad
\operatorname{argmin}F_D=C_D
=\{z:\langle z,g\rangle\le0\ \forall g\in D\}.
$$

对每个固定序列，限制到这个模型后的上确界等于原来的 $\mathcal W_n(h)$。证明使用严格光滑化、增加高度坐标、小扰动和维数压缩得到 simplex，并同时处理分母中的最小点距离。结论是**上确界相等**，没有声称每条原始轨迹都由某个有限参数 simplex 精确表示，也没有给出对所有步长统一的条件数或逼近速度。完整最小点集合是极锥，不能一律把分母换成 $\|x_0\|^2$。[已接收的表示与七步结果](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/fact_graph/facts/1e0bf3f41457d8e4.md)

另一表示采用

$$
\Phi_{K,\ell}(X)=\min_{Y\in K}\left\{\langle\ell,Y\rangle+\tfrac12\|X-Y\|^2\right\},
\qquad \nabla\Phi_{K,\ell}(X)=X-P_K(X-\ell).
$$

其中 $K$ 是包含零的紧多面体，至多有 $n+2$ 个顶点，且 $\langle\ell,Y\rangle>0$ 对所有非零 $Y\in K$ 成立；取 $X_0\perp\ell$。该模型唯一最小点为零，并保持完整有限序列上确界。它与 $\nabla F_D=P_D$ 的模型不能混用。[已接收的 affine-height 表示](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/fact_graph/facts/d8b59c7d92425121.md)

终端化简则把上界问题写成完整原始插值行

$$
Q_{ij}=f_i-f_j+\langle g_i,x_j-x_i\rangle+\tfrac12\|g_i-g_j\|^2\le0
$$

的非负组合与 PSD 二次型。可以选取没有 incoming-terminal multipliers 的紧证书；该选择没有删除 queried prefix 内的约束。终点平方可精确剥离，剩下的是原始前缀空间上的完整 PSD 条件。这是后续系数搜索的依据。[已接收的终端化简](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/fact_graph/facts/1252b8823bb5a290.md)

**三、最清楚的主目标缺口**

目前重点考察一个轻微偏离 Silver 参数的步长族。对每个 enclosing integer $m\ge1536$，定义

$$
\varepsilon_m=\frac1{4m},\quad \beta=1-\varepsilon_m,\quad
c=(1+\sqrt2)(1+\varepsilon_m),\quad a=\frac\beta c,
$$

$$
H_0^{(m)}=(),\qquad
H_{j+1}^{(m)}=(H_j^{(m)},1+ac^j,H_j^{(m)}),\quad 0\le j<m.
$$

记 $S_j=\sum H_j^{(m)}$、$n_m=2^m-1$、$D_m=1+2S_m$。每个子块始终使用同一个 enclosing $m$，不能按子块深度重置 $\varepsilon_m$。

已接收的算术比较给出

$$
\frac{B_{n_m}}{D_m}<\frac{47}{50}.
$$

因此，只要最终能够对全部原始函数类、对所有充分大的 $m$，证明

$$
\boxed{\mathcal W_{n_m}(H_m^{(m)})\le\frac{17}{16D_m},}
$$

就会得到

$$
\liminf_{n\to\infty}\frac{\mathcal W_n^\star}{U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}}
\le\frac{799}{800}<1,
$$

从而否定原始渐近匹配命题。这里的全函数类上界**尚未证明**。若取得 $(1+C/m)/D_m$ 的统一上界，其中 $C$ 为绝对常数，也足够形成反例。这里的起始深度阈值可以大于 1536，但必须独立于具体函数和起点。[已接收的基准比较和条件桥梁](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/fact_graph/facts/931b9653ce0f7201.md)

现有七步成果准确地是：对每个同样的 $m\ge1536$，

$$
\mathcal W_7(H_3^{(m)})=\frac1{1+2S_3}.
$$

更强地，对任意紧凸体 $D\ni0$、任意维数、起点和 $d\ge1$，定义

$$
U_d(x)=\max_{g\in D}(2\langle x,g\rangle-d\|g\|^2),
$$

则实际七步复合满足

$$
U_d(T_{H_3^{(m)}}x)\le U_{d+2S_3}(x).
$$

证明把两个实际子块、桥接点及辅助投影的约束合并，并逐项支付余项；Huber 实例给出下界等号。当前还缺能覆盖所有层数的定量组合机制。原目标也不要求先证明最强的 all-$d$ 传播：任何直接支付真实初始距离的完整上界都可以。

**四、近期候选推导的进展**

这些结果均未被正式接收；它们用于定位下一步，而不是作为新定理直接引用。

| 路线 | 当前候选结果 | 仍缺什么 |
|---|---|---|
| 奇对称三段函数 | 完整草稿给出 $(1+21/(4m))/D_m$，并经过一次独立分析审阅 | 仍是指定标量函数族，尚非全函数类上界 |
| 两侧不同截断高度 | 当较小与较大截断之比至少为 $c-2$ 时，已有完整 all-start 草稿，同样给出该上界 | 比值更小时的全部轨迹未覆盖 |
| 较小截断侧的尾部终点 | 草稿覆盖所有此类终点：上界 $1/D_m$；有穿越零点的轨迹实际上只能穿越一次，且严格小于 $2/(3D_m)$ | 其他终点仍需原始半径估计 |
| 一般双截断函数的梯度 | 最新报告覆盖至多两次穿越：$p_{\rm end}<R/(2d_j)$，并对多次穿越给出截断相关界 | 至少三次穿越、起点半径较小、终点位于截断前的情形仍未解决 |

这里的标量函数族固定为

$$
g(\sigma r)=\sigma\min\{B_\sigma,\max(\kappa r,r-(1-\kappa))\},
\quad 0<\kappa\le1,\quad B_\sigma\ge\kappa,
$$

其中 $\sigma\in\{-1,1\}$、$r\ge0$，并取 $f(x)=\int_0^x g(t)\,dt$。同一函数用于全部步骤；$R=|x_0|$，$d_j=ac^j$，$p_{\rm end}=|g(x_{\rm end})|$。

其中一个有用的新结构是“最后一次穿越”的支付：若完整子块最终落在较大截断侧，则最后一次穿越来自较小截断侧。设其层级为 $k$，完整后缀步长和为 $T$，便有

$$
p_{\rm end}\le\frac{d_kB_{\min}}{1+T}
\le\frac{d_kB_{\min}}{1+S_k}<(c-2)B_{\min}.
$$

较小截断侧尾部的结果进一步用实际前后缀步长乘积排除了两次以上穿越，再把唯一穿越的代价付回原始 $R$。这比只检查根部的一次特定跳转更广，但尚未补齐整个双截断族。[完整尾部候选](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/shared_evidence/b726f6dc7baa6b7b.md)；[最新梯度部分结果](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/shared_evidence/21e479d3aa05eed3.md)

系数证书方面，已得到若干**指定形式的排除推导**：源端三项约束、移动下标的源端流量、远端根部的前缀约束，以及最后一行的时钟矩约束。这些约束排除了具体的共同列和 PSD 余项选择，包括全部兼容的 skew 补全。投影 $H_4$ 路线中，若干起点约束和拼接权重的分配也被完整二次型中的负方向排除。它们没有排除一般证书锥，也没有给出真正违反目标不等式的合法函数。[一般终端接口及两种列的障碍](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/shared_evidence/8bcf300ff83e2288.md)

**五、Gray 与有限域路线：计数已修订，完整轨迹仍待构造**

这一方向用二进制 Gray code 和 $\mathbb F_2^m$ 上的 Walsh characters 构造高维顶点，再要求这些顶点成为实际梯度。它已经用到了有限域字符正交性、按二进制位展开的有限生成多项式，以及按时间顺序计算的前缀和。这些方法目前贡献了可分析的几何结构，尚未提供原问题所需的统一下界。

对固定、线性无关的顶点 $v_0,\ldots,v_n$，令 $V$ 的列为这些顶点，并定义

$$
P_i=\sum_{k<i}h_kv_k,\quad A_{ij}=\langle P_i,v_j\rangle,\quad
G_{ij}=\langle v_i,v_j\rangle,
$$
$$
C_{ij}=A_{ij}-A_{ii}+G_{ij}-G_{ii},\quad
K_i=A_{ii}+G_{ii},\quad c_n=A_{nn}+\tfrac12G_{nn}.
$$

初始化势 $p_i=\langle x_0,v_i\rangle$ 不是函数值。指定的完整梯度轨迹合法，当且仅当所有原始有序对和零点约束同时满足

$$
p_j-p_i\le C_{ij},\qquad p_i\ge K_i.
$$

此时目标函数为 $F_D$，$D=\operatorname{conv}\{0,v_0,\ldots,v_n\}$，终点函数值为 $p_n-c_n$，完整最小点集合为 $C_D=\{z:V^\top z\le0\}$。必须使用到 $C_D$ 的真实距离。

已有候选推导将全部合法初始化的归一化收益写成以下凸 perspective：

$$
\begin{aligned}
 \sup\;&2F_n-2\tau c_n,\\
 &F=V^\top b,\quad \tau\ge0,\\
 &F_j-F_i\le\tau C_{ij},\quad F_i\ge\tau K_i,\\
 &V^\top z_\star\le0,\quad \|b-z_\star\|^2\le\tau.
\end{aligned}
$$

对 $\tau>0$，除以 $\tau$ 恢复原始初始化及一个合法最小点；反方向则用真实最近最小点构造 perspective 变量。由此得到的是“所有合法收益与零的并集”的上确界。$\tau=0$ 的可行零点并不证明指定轨迹可以实现，数值返回的 $z_\star$ 也不能自动称为最近最小点。[完整有限维推导](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/gray_full_perspective_snapshot_20261008/DERIVATION.md)

10 月 3 日发现，原阻尼参数为 4 的稿件把第二级步数计错。若前两级径向比为 $x,y$，实际半径应为

$$
\sigma_k=x^{\lceil k/2\rceil}y^{\lfloor(k+2)/4\rfloor}.
$$

旧稿误用了 $\lfloor(k+1)/4\rfloor$。因此在 $k=4q+2$ 处，正确系数是 $xy\theta^q$，其中 $\theta=x^2y$；生成多项式中相应系数也必须补上 $y$。

10 月 8 日完成了一份独立修订稿，逐项追踪了这个更改对有限生成多项式、前缀和、正反向约束以及证书分母的影响。在保持原阻尼参数 4 和全部顶点不变的前提下，修订稿重新推出 $0<x<y<1$，并恢复指数包络。取 $N=2^m$、$i=N/4-1$、$s=\sigma_i$、$t=\sigma_n=s^4$，对包含正负核权重的实际前缀量

$$
J=\langle P_i,w_i+w_n\rangle
 +(1+s^3)\langle P_n-P_i,w_n\rangle,\qquad v_k=\sigma_kw_k,
$$

稿件给出 $J>(7/15)S_{m-2}$。保留原来唯一非零有序对乘子 $2t/(s+t)$ 后，完整修订推导得到

$$
\sup\left(\{0\}\cup
\left\{\frac{2F_D(x_n)}{\operatorname{dist}(x_0,C_D)^2}:
x_0\text{ 实现指定完整顶点轨迹}\right\}\right)
<\frac{343}{333D_m}<\frac{17}{16D_m},\qquad m\ge1536.
$$

**这个常数已在修订稿中重新闭合，但仍未被正式接收。**完整修订稿已读完；本次总结没有进行新的独立数学核查。该结论只涉及一个固定几何和一条指定轨迹的全部合法初始化，不覆盖该几何上的任意轨迹，更不覆盖全部光滑凸函数；它甚至不主张指定轨迹的合法初始化集合非空。因此它是一个候选构造的受限障碍，不能放进第三节的全函数类上界位置。[完整修订稿](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/gray_count_repair_20261008/REPORT.md)

源码检查还发现，旧数值几何通过逐步累乘生成半径，没有使用上述错误的封闭计数公式。后续公共平移程序读取这些保存数组；这只厘清了错误传播范围，没有重新验证旧浮点结果。

阻尼改为 2 时，已有另一份完整候选报告：显式的逐相邻约束取等势函数在一个原始远距离约束处失败；保留指定两顶点的放松问题却允许收益大于 $69/(64D_m)>17/(16D_m)$。这说明这一对子约束不足以排除该变化后的构造，尚未给出满足全部原始约束的真实高维轨迹。[阻尼 2 的报告](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/shared_evidence/a37b81ea81b53910.md)

**六、数值工作实际说明了什么**

最近的计算都有单独预算和校准。以下仅列会影响下一步判断的结果。

在同一 enclosing $m=1536$ 的有限 $H_3,H_4$ 上，以 $q=17/[16(1+2\sum h)]$ 搜索完整原始 dual，两种模式——自由终端参数 $\pi$ 和固定 $\pi=\sqrt q$——都返回了数值解。因而没有观察到这两类的一般可行性分离。H4 自由模式返回的 $\pi\approx-7.02\times10^{-12}$ 及其负乘子原样保留，没有被改写成精确零；其他模式也保留了小负特征值。这些不是精确证书。[有限 dual 诊断](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/terminal_mass_interior_discovery_20261003/HANDOFF.md)

10 月 3 日的一批计算直接最大化投影端点不等式的违反量

$$
V=U_1(T_hx_0)-U_{1+2\sum h}(x_0),\qquad \|x_0\|=1,
$$

纳入全部原始投影法向约束，得到：

| 有限算例 | 返回的 $V$ | 求解状态 |
|---|---:|---|
| $H_3$ | $1.0874\times10^{-9}$ | optimal |
| $H_4$ | $2.4904\times10^{-9}$ | optimal_inaccurate |

两项已知校准均通过，但上述生产值都接近零。H4 的最小原始 Gram 特征值为 $-1.5756\times10^{-10}$，最大原始法向残差为 $3.7285\times10^{-10}$，dual stationarity 缺陷约为 $1.8965\times10^{-8}$。因此这批结果没有给出可信的精确反例，也没有证明 $H_4$ 不等式。这两个生产算例的原始 Gram 都含小负特征值，坐标重建被跳过，没有通过裁剪特征值制造“实际轨迹”。[H3 原始摘要](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/projection_u_primal_discovery_20261003/03_H3/summary.json)；[H4 原始摘要](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/projection_u_primal_discovery_20261003/04_H4/summary.json)

此前 Gray 的几个有限字典算例只产生了带正残差的数值势函数；优化公共平移后也没有提供所需的真实下界见证。有限字典随维数改变，不能外推为完整 $2^m$ 维构造的合法性或渐近结论。

10 月 8 日的新探索原本准备对上述 perspective 优化所有初始化，范围大于旧的公共平移搜索。空序列校准返回 $0.9999999991995898$，通过预设标准；无合法正尺度初始化的第二个校准返回 $9.512658923071169\times10^{-8}$，但求解状态是 optimal_inaccurate，没有满足预先固定的 optimal 要求。程序按约定停止，四个 Gray 正式算例均未运行。这个停止既不支持也不否定 Gray 构造的合法性；本轮没有新增 Gray 数值结论。[校准停止报告](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/gray_full_perspective_snapshot_20261008/REPORT.md)

**七、恢复后需要调整的方向**

当前关键缺口已经具体化：反例方向缺少所有层数、全部函数类上的统一上界；肯定方向缺少对任意步长可构造且损失足够小的高维对手。有限表示定理提供了研究接口，没有替我们完成这两个估计中的任何一个。

1. Gray 的已知计数错误已有完整修订稿，恢复时不必重新发现或重做这次修订。是否安排正式核查，应由它对后续方向的实际用途决定。阻尼 2 的重点仍是全部原始约束的同时可行性与真实距离；先前校准未过不能算一次正式算例的失败。
2. 若继续尝试否定原命题，应以 $17/(16D_m)$ 的完整上界为交付标准。七步精确传播可以保留为基块，但当前没有到任意深度的闭合规律。最近完整报告只排除了两种指定的 $H_4$ 拼接权重分配，不能据此排除整个证书方法。[两类拼接障碍](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/shared_evidence/e5264ef28cade979.md)
3. 可以保留概率论方向作为尚未分配的思路：将投影法向约束的非负乘子解释为从终点到辅助投影点的流量，并用时钟矩条件寻找跨层的整体二次型估计。现阶段只有未独立核查的讨论线索，没有可用的全深度定理；必须交付新的定量支付机制才值得继续，不能仅将旧缺口改写成概率语言。
4. 标量双截断路线还有至少三次穿越、较小原始半径、截断前终点的具体缺口。它可以作为局部问题保留，但其完整解也不会自动给出全维上界。该路线最新长报告尚未由主代理通读，不宜在恢复前把部分结论扩大。

为避免额度继续消耗在相近的局部修补上，下一轮宜先选定一项能直接检验上述核心缺口的交付物，并明确失败后转向条件。当前仅记录这些选择，不分配新任务。

**八、暂停状态与接续入口**

这项工作确实使用了 Danus：项目、共享结果、研究轮次及核查接收记录由它管理，研究后端使用 Codex；另有明确额度的直接 Codex 分析。最近两项直接研究使用 gpt-6-astra / ultra。已接收结论与直接研究草稿始终分开记录。

Goal 已于 10 月 8 日再次标记为 PAUSED，原问题仍未解决。四个 Danus 研究员均无存活研究进程，两名本轮直接研究代理都已完成。最新数值批次已关闭，未用的作业额度已注销；本次整理没有开启新的研究、数值计算、独立写作模型或正式核查。只有你明确恢复后才会继续调整和调用。

恢复入口是本总结、[原始完整问题](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/PROBLEM.md) 和 [10 月 8 日暂停接续索引](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/PAUSED_HANDOFF_20261008.md)。索引保存证据位置、已完成修订、尚未读完的材料、关闭的预算及先前中止的任务。10 月 3 日的总结和旧源稿均原样保留，作为历史记录。

# R14: close the weak history induction using non-unit left retention and real cross-subtree rows

Fresh allocation: ONE native gpt-6-astra / ultra invocation, at most TWO substantive analytical approaches; mathematical programs, generated arrays, solvers and formal submissions ZERO. No additional agents/models, old grants, worker/service changes or automatic renewal. Parent owns all ledgers/shared administration. Write complete PROOF_ATTEMPT.md and HANDOFF.md in this directory only, label new math UNVERIFIED, report meaningful progress and final hashes/counts, then stop.

The human explicitly authorizes direct mathematical research and wants an actual all-depth/all-word closure inspired by Zhang--Jiang, not another equivalent formulation or fixed-horizon model. Goal ACTIVE/OPEN; consult OFF. Preserve exact W=2R, all-integer B_n, all finite-dimensional convex smooth functions, original selected minimizer/radius and actual endpoint.

Read OPERATOR.md, the complete PROBLEM.md, R13_FINAL_HANDOFF_20261009.md, the COMPLETE relaxed_history_for_pd_join_20261009_r13/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md (SHA 1bd04c98681c45cbfad72ade79e3c49a1fa9ed1031e9f72e3fc048941fdf3688), and COMPLETE R12_RELAXED_PD_JOIN_LEAD_20261009.md (SHA f7a80cbc05ee2da4b168f61fbc307817550865fa94978fcbfe067f1dae125c09). Read R11_VARIABLE_INTERVAL_CERTIFICATE_LEAD_20261009.md Section 5 before using interval certificates. Higher primitive positivity is unavailable.

For ONE enclosing m>=1536 define epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon, c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+epsilon), a=beta/c, H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), S_j=sum H_j, C_j=1+S_j. EVERY child inherits these same parameters. The exact target is to prove, for all sufficiently large m and EVERY 1<=j<m, the full-class actual history bound

    E_j = 2 sum h_i(f_i-f_end) - sum h_i(h_i-1)||g_i||^2
          - [(7/10) C_j^2-C_j] ||g_end||^2 >= 0.

The parent PD join has an explicit uniform positive determinant under exactly this input. The actual leaf is available. A valid uniform proof closes W(H_m)<=1/[1+(32/17)S_m] and therefore the original negative resolution via accepted 931b9653ce0f7201. This conditional path is not itself a solution.

R13's ACTUAL asymmetric-Huber history proves that a weak-history join retaining alpha>=1 times the weak LEFT residual and adding only nonnegative terms is impossible. It also excludes the attempted full three-vector bridge/right-row family. Both children AND parent remain strictly positive on this witness, so the input itself is not refuted. Do not repeat that family or merely retune its nonnegative coefficients.

Your first concrete construction should allow 0<=alpha<1 on the weak left residual and use genuine original cross-left/right rows to restore the required physical function-value coefficients. For example, a weighted sum of Q_(i,y) over left physical i has the needed positive left f_i coefficients; its complete gradient remainder, including temporal moments and all diagonal terms, must be retained. This example is a lead, NOT an asserted PSD construction. Use the full original gradient space rather than three independent endpoint vectors when necessary. Choose actual coefficients, prove every coefficient nonnegative and the FULL remainder PSD, and close the induction quantitatively on all inherited j<m. A useful simultaneous invariant is permitted only if its leaf and complete preservation are proved. Returning another uninstantiated feasibility problem is not closure.

As a distinct second approach, you may quantify the whole-tree retained standard-history identity from R13:

    E_P^std = E_L^std + B E_R^std
               + ell(A_v+A_y+Q_vy+k Q_yv)
               + Delta (||q||^2-||p||^2),
    ell=(C+k)/(C-k), B=1+2ell,
    Delta=C^2(1+r)(r^2+2r-1)/(1-r), r=k/C.

All rows are original. The signed child energy and positive Delta times a possibly negative gradient difference MUST be retained. A tree-weighted coercivity or telescoping estimate would need explicit constants that cover epsilon=1/(4m), every j<m and terminal loss at most (3/10)C_j^2. At epsilon=0, weak history is strictly coercive for each fixed j, but its compactness proof supplies no uniform constants. Reproving fixed-j continuity or exchanging quantifiers is insufficient. A quantitative growing-depth actual counterexample is also decisive for this input, but does not solve the original minimax problem by itself.

Known exclusions: no unit/full weak-left-retention join; no positive interval-mode/diagonal-slack tuning; no fixed-depth absorbed negative-history family for all large enclosing m; no farther-original-minimizer absorption in cone projections (even with nonbinding compact caps). A growing-depth active-cap or nonhomogeneous actual function remains outside those exclusions. The increasing-profile Huber inner-phase family from R13 passes its prescribed residual and must not be relabelled as a counterexample.

The result must be an actual uniform proof, an actual growing-depth input obstruction with all physical data, or a precise failure of the NEW construction after trying to instantiate it. Do not return only a new potential definition, a negative direction of a free matrix represented as a real function, a fixed-depth table, or a scoped theorem advertised as the original answer. If a complete original-target proof emerges, write all assumptions/constants for a separate finished-object adversarial audit; do not submit formally in this invocation.

# Cyclic interpolation tools for the legal-trajectory lane

Main-authored source triage. Optional support, not a new fact, instruction to
solve an LP, or replacement of the current tasks. Checked September27,2026.

## Strict finite matching and smoothing

del Barrio, Cuesta-Albertos, Hallin and Matran,
[Smooth Cyclically Monotone Interpolation, explicit v1](https://arxiv.org/html/1806.01238v1).
Proposition2.1 relates finite cyclic monotonicity to supporting weights; its
strict version corresponds to uniqueness of the matching. Theorem2.2 and
Corollary2.4 retain Assumption(A). For bounded output vectors, the latter
constructs an interpolating Moreau envelope with gradient Lipschitz bound
1/epsilon_0, where epsilon_0 is half the minimum strict supporting gap.
Equations2.11-2.12 formulate gap selection and a circulation dual.

Potential use: test whether a concrete proposed legal repair has a uniform
supporting-gap bound, rather than fixing one violated pair. This is only a
possible construction tool. Relabeling gradients by an optimal matching
does not preserve a prescribed GD trajectory automatically. A small gap may
cause an unacceptable smoothness/normalization loss. The optimum jet, actual
terminal value and true initial distance still need explicit checks. Use the
strict stated theorem with its hypotheses; no unqualified extension statement
is imported here, and no minimum-cycle algorithm or optimal Lipschitz theorem
is asserted beyond the displayed source statements.

## Value-free interpolation and the metric it actually bounds

Lee and Seiler,
[Finite Step Performance of First-order Methods Using Interpolation Conditions Without Function Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2005.01825).
Lemma4 states the transformed cyclic-monotonicity criterion for the stated
strongly convex regime0<mu<L. Lemma8 identifies the cone generated by I-P,
with P a permutation matrix, with zero-excess doubly hyperdominant matrices.
Theorem1's displayed performance metric is iterate distance, not last-iterate
objective gap; the paper's trajectory reconstruction also has explicit
feasibility/nondegeneracy conditions.

Possible use is a combinatorial organization of ALL cycle constraints, not
a free import of its performance theorem. For the present convex objective
problem, derive the exact needed specialization from accepted1252b8823bb5a290
and keep function-value completion. An exact value-free reformulation alone
is already known and is not a target-specific lower bound. No finite distance
bound, strongly convex rate or lossless-S-procedure slogan closes this goal.

## Retrieval boundary

The old ULB bitstream redirected to INVALID_ID. The arXiv v1 PDF retrieval
returned Internal Error; the explicit v1 HTML and abstract worked and were
read. Searching that HTML for Karp returned no matching text. The note uses
the displayed strict-gap/circulation statements, not an unobserved named
algorithm. Lee-Seiler's full PDF was available. No software was installed.

# Directed graph tools: exact matching still required

Main-authored optional source triage, September27,2026. No theorem import,
new assignment, solver allowance or accepted fact.

Yibin Zhao, *Fully Dynamic Spectral and Cut Sparsifiers for Directed Graphs*,
[ICALP2026 paper](https://drops.dagstuhl.de/storage/00lipics/lipics-vol374-icalp2026/html/LIPIcs.ICALP.2026.157/LIPIcs.ICALP.2026.157.html):
Definition2 controls the directed bilinear error relative to the associated
undirected Laplacian. Lemma10 identifies preservation of each vertex's degree
imbalance. Theorem3 allows auxiliary vertices, with guarantees after their
Schur elimination, and assumes polynomially bounded weights. The balanced-cut
results apply to cuts satisfying their specified balance condition.

Potential connection for max/max4, still UNMATCHED: actual interpolation
coefficients form a directed array and value cancellation imposes flows.
Test whether any exact flow-preserving operation also controls the physical
chronological moment terms in the FULL objective remainder. Degree balance
alone is not that conclusion. Auxiliary graph vertices do not automatically
represent legal extra queries; arbitrary multiplier weights do not meet a
polynomial-bound hypothesis automatically. An approximation theorem needs
an explicit error budget below the original constant threshold, including
all repeated levels and singular kernels. Do not remove queried constraints
under the label of graph elimination.

Cohen et al., *Solving Directed Laplacian Systems in Nearly-Linear Time
through Sparse LU Factorizations*,
[full paper](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1811.10722): Section4 explicitly notes that
the symmetric part of a directed Schur complement can grow relative to the
original. Lemma4.3 gives a conditional general-matrix bound; Lemma4.2 supplies
a bounded-elimination statement for the paper's alpha-RCDD subsets of an
Eulerian Laplacian. This is not a general monotonicity principle for arbitrary
certificate matrices or arbitrary eliminated blocks.

Peng and Song, *Sparsified Block Elimination for Directed Laplacians*,
[full paper](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2111.10257): Theorem3.3 concerns strongly
connected Eulerian Laplacians with an alpha-RCDD block. Section4 uses augmented
matrices to encode and bound errors of repeated block elimination. A possible
method analogy is explicit storage of errors across recursion, but neither
its Laplacian hypotheses nor its algorithmic approximation guarantee has
been matched to the present GD remainder. No claim of constant preservation
or full-certificate positivity follows merely from the analogy.

Disposition: keep as a source lead if a concrete coefficient/cut identity
exposes the required structure. Continue the existing exact calculations
first; a generic Laplacian reformulation with the hard PSD inequality still
unproved is a blocked route, not a solved bridge.

# Discrete Hardy tools for the chronological-kernel interface

Read-only primary-source search by main, 2026-09-27. This note is contextual
strategy, not an accepted Danus fact, a matched theorem, or a proof.

1. Laurent Miclo, *An example of application of discrete Hardy's inequalities*,
[author PDF](https://perso.math.univ-toulouse.fr/miclo/files/2012/04/Hardy.pdf).
Proposition1 on PDF page3 concerns positive sequence weights mu,nu and the
best squared-norm constant A for weighted prefix sums. Its tail-prefix
quantity B satisfies B<=A<=4B. The paper applies such estimates to spectral
gaps of birth-and-death processes. These are quantitatively explicit but
factor-loss bounds. They are not sharp constant-one domination for our
time-inhomogeneous kernel. A claimed application must exhibit the actual
weights, boundary conditions and transformation, preserving terminal clock,
constant column and all signed remainder terms. The presence of a birth
representation alone is not enough. Total positivity likewise does not
remove the loss or prove covariance domination.

2. Ali Barki, *Sharp Weighted Discrete p-Hardy Inequality and Stability*,
[arXiv2501.00299v1](https://arxiv.org/html/2501.00299v1).
Section2 treats two positive measures. Proposition2.1 bounds the best Hardy
constant between B and p^p/(p-1)^(p-1) times B. Proposition2.3 separately treats
the zero-sum sequence class; Remark2.2 explains why this distinction matters.
Section3's Theorem3.3 builds an optimal graph Hardy weight from a positive
superharmonic function, assuming a connected graph, finitely supported
nonnegative potential, properness and bounded neighbor oscillation.
This is a possible source of an exact supersolution method, not a theorem
already matched to our finite, horizon-dependent, signed birth-coordinate
matrix. Any use needs an explicit finite specialization and boundary proof;
neither graph positivity nor bounded oscillation is automatic here.

Current worker-local-square context is publicly mirrored in
shared_evidence/3d57065c9de5e34b.md, with boundary extension d6e2788596a23c4f.
The actual unanswered question is uniform bulk/right-boundary coefficient
signs and the full alternating constant load, for every original m>=128.
Simple diagonal dominance already fails on the displayed row2 limit. The
first vanishing coefficient r1 scales as1/m, so a fixed positive lower bound
from m128 is false. A Hardy comparison may be useful only if its quantified
loss fits an actual available margin, or an exact supersolution replaces the
lossy comparison. It must not be called constant preserving by analogy.

Search boundary: no posted solution to the exact user benchmark was sought.
The additional BNU-hosted paper81.pdf returned Internal Error on opening and
is NOT relied on. No optimizer, kernel evaluation, installation or verifier
call was made. This note does not renew any resource allocation.

# Primary source leads for nonlocal original-row transport

2026-10-02. Main source retrieval and scope record, not new mathematics.
Original exact-profile Goal ACTIVE/OPEN. Sol/max. All existing worker
allocations and the consumed ONE formal attempt remain unchanged.

## Directed degree-preserving elimination

Cohen, Kelner, Kyng, Peebles, Peng, Rao and Sidford,
Solving Directed Laplacian Systems in Nearly-Linear Time through Sparse
LU Factorizations, arXiv:1811.10722v1.
Primary full paper: https://arxiv.org/pdf/1811.10722 .

Read the introduction's elimination discussion, Section3 Algorithm4,
Section4 Definition4.1 and Lemmas4.2--4.4. For Eulerian directed
Laplacians, eliminating a vertex creates an incoming-to-outgoing biclique.
Their nonnegative equal-mass coupling preserves weighted degrees while
matching that dense object in expectation. Their analysis also identifies
Schur-complement growth; arbitrary elimination is not protected by a
uniform constant, and controlled sets/norms are needed. The main output
is an approximate LU/linear-system guarantee, not an exact PSD
certificate for an arbitrary signed symmetric matrix.

## Chordal positive/semidefinite completion

Grone, Johnson, Sa and Wolkowicz, Positive Definite Completions of Partial
Hermitian Matrices, Linear Algebra and its Applications58 (1984),109--124.
DOI: https://doi.org/10.1016/0024-3795(84)90207-6 .
Author-hosted original paper:
https://www.math.uwaterloo.ca/~hwolkowi/henry/reports/pdcomplGJSW84.pdf .

Read Section2 definitions, Proposition2, Section6 Theorem7 and Section7.
The theorem concerns partially specified Hermitian matrices: compatible
positive data on specified cliques admit completion for chordal patterns.
The paper also treats the semidefinite case. Unspecified entries are
free completion variables. This does not assert positivity of an already
fully specified matrix, or realizability of a completion by a prescribed
nonnegative multiplier/flow map.

## Missing project interfaces, inference only

These are possible organizing tools for NEXT planning; no imported
Danus fact or successful law follows. A worker would still need an
explicit mapping to every original interpolation row, correct value
flow, nonnegative signs, attainable free entries, all initial mixed
coordinates, and a paid whole-word constant. No such mapping is supplied
by this main-agent note. Random averaging of illegal primal trajectories
or graph completion outside the actual multiplier cone remains invalid.

Current max75 has at most3 explicit laws and may use source orientation
within that allocation; this note does not authorize a fourth law or
extra invocation. max3r67/max4r70 remain unchanged, as does max2r64's
SAME in-flight formal call (used1/limit1, remaining0). All mathematical
program/numerical/optimizer/DP/replay allowances remain zero.

## Retrieval record

Two standard-theorem web queries, primary-source opens and exact section
lookups were used. The first Wisconsin PDF endpoint and a2008 publisher
open returned Internal Error. The1984 original PDF was recovered through
the author's verified publications-page link. The2008 text was not
imported. No posted solution to the exact benchmark was searched.
No new theorem/proof, formal call, model-backed auxiliary, mathematical
evaluation, publication, finalization, revocation or shutdown occurred.

# Source methods revisited, 2026-10-08

These are primary-source method leads, not Danus facts or imported proofs of the target. No posted solution to the exact benchmark was sought.

## Dense interpolation multipliers

[Chikhi, arXiv2609.13765v2](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.13765v2), Section4, constructs nonnegative interpolation multipliers from two monotone scalar sequences for a specially balanced constant schedule. The source also distinguishes terminal smooth-gradient performance from the proximal objective evaluated at a different point. It was already inspected on October1; there is no literature-newness claim.

The new root work allowed varying steps and nonnegative diagonal slack, and derived a sufficient17/16 terminal penalty. The independently checked extension is in dense_history_search_20261008_r2/REPORT.md. Its entire specified monotone A/D family fails the actual hierarchy's central bridge. Thus merely varying those two scalar sequences cannot supply the desired full-class upper. Non-diagonal remainders remain outside the exclusion.

## Laplacian sum-of-squares structure

[Bok and Altschuler, arXiv2412.05497](https://arxiv.org/html/2412.05497), Section3.2 and Lemma14, prove positivity of a recursive slack matrix using a Laplacian and its Schur complement. This is a method lead for retaining structured cross terms.

The source analyzes proximal gradient updates and the original Silver schedule. This project's projection-gradient dynamics and relaxed proximal interpretation do not automatically match those updates. No theorem or coefficient table was transplanted. Any future use needs an exact update/criterion translation and a new identity for the altered same-m word.

Retrieval notes: the guessed v3 HTML path returned404; the unversioned arXiv HTML was available. One optional web request failed at transport level and the PMLR PDF attempt returned an internal error; no unavailable file was treated as read. The relevant arXiv Section3.2 was later read successfully.

# Source normalization note — 2026-09-26

This is a bibliographic reconciliation, not a newly accepted project fact.

In [Ye–Liu v2](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.09152v2), equation (1) divides by (L/2)||x_0-x_*||^2, so its R_n equals this project's W_n. The attachment uses a different R convention. Its W definition and target (P)/(Q) remain unchanged.

Lemma 2.2, equation (6), states prefactor 1/[4(1+s_tail)]. However, immediately after equation (12), the constructed gap is D^2/[4(1+s_tail)], the initial distance is 1, and the paper explicitly drops a factor 2 when stating the lemma. The stronger project checkpoint coefficient 1/[2(1+s_tail)] is supported by that pre-weakening construction when all its hypotheses are matched. Cite that calculation rather than the literal lemma statement.

Theorem 1.1's displayed normalization should not be mechanically doubled. Keep explicit constants separate from the existential exponent constant.

Use one-based H_t=h_{t-1} when importing the paper's checkpoints.

## Additional literature scope, checked 2026-09-26

[Liu–Chen–Jiang–Wang, arXiv:2609.11788v1](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.11788v1), Remark 1.2, limits optimality to recursively generated classes. Sections 2.1–2.2 explicitly distinguish ambient primitive and s-composable certificate classes despite their treewise scalar correspondence. Its phase analysis is background for the benchmark, not an all-schedule minimax proof. Any use of its asymmetric phase for this exact benchmark requires matching the recurrences and normalization.

[Zhao, arXiv:2609.11784v1](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.11784v1) proves s-composability for a particular balanced constant-step family. Its stated certificate and sum-product hypotheses must be matched before reuse; it does not assert unrestricted schedule optimality.

Neither paper has been imported as a new Danus fact. The conditional seed-amplification candidate in research/max4_primitive_seed_amplification.md includes its needed scalar argument and leaves the required seed hypothesis unmet.

## Certificate-memory methods, screened 2026-09-26

September27 duality follow-up: SOURCE_DUAL_ARC_LEAD_20260927.md records primary checks of Shu--Wang2609.03281v1 (full slack congruence plus separate signs; tridiagonal reduced-certificate sufficient case) and Yoon--Grimmer2605.02231v2 Theorem4 (noncrossing arc characterization for optimal vertex fixed-point algorithms). The latter concerns nonexpansive residual minimax, not this GD intrinsic cone. Its dual multiplier graphs are distinct from the primal coordinate-flow graphs in C35/C39. Neither source proves general primitive reversal or the original constant-profile target.

September27 supplementary screen: intake SOURCE_LEADS_20260927_0020.md records primary-source pointers and explicit applicability limits for Liu--Ye arXiv:2609.26873v1 (strong-convex repair/bridge construction) and Bok--Altschuler's proximal silver analysis (Section3.2, Laplacian Schur-complement slack). These are optional mechanism leads, not constant-profile results or new facts. Strong-convex transfer losses, composite-operator hypotheses, opposite interpolation sign and the absence of a general re-selection theorem must all be retained.

The intake HISTORICAL_CERTIFICATE_MEMORY_LEAD.md records a bounded primary-source screen of arXiv:2207.01363, arXiv:1705.03615 and arXiv:1812.02397, with exact section pointers and scope limits. These are methodology leads for retained boundary information, not imported facts or posted resolutions of this benchmark. In particular, no conjectural completeness claim or unproved reset of an inherited filter state may be used in the project's proof.

# Phase-sensitive seed amplification: source and scope check

Contextual source screen,2026-09-27; not an accepted Danus theorem.

[Liu,Chen,Jiang,Wang,arXiv2609.11788v1](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.11788v1)
uses leaf count N. Proposition2.5 identifies U_N with the symmetric
recursive primitive scalar. Section6 reciprocates OBS-F rates into W_N
with W_1=1 and join A(u,w)=(4u+w+sqrt(w*w+8u*w))/2. Theorem6.3 gives a
positive Lipschitz periodic asymptotic profile for N^p times the OBS-F rate,
uniform over dyadic blocks. Theorem6.5 states nonconstancy and strict
separation from its homogeneous support barrier. Remark1.2 explicitly
limits optimality to recursively generated classes. Application here
requires matching U_N=P_(N-1),source W_N=B_(N-1),p=our rho, normalization,
and every index shift. Neither that paper's theorem names nor its reported
formalization are treated as verifier acceptance by this project.

C72 d4ccd3fd9ce605f1 proves a DRAFT sufficient amplification threshold
A_seed>(r_seed+1)^rho by replacing primitive leaves and comparing through
one GLOBAL limsup. That criterion deliberately avoids a phase theorem.
It does not establish necessity of that threshold, or rule out useful
non-dyadic seeds below the power envelope. Main's new proposed question is
whether an exact phase-resolved comparison gives a strictly weaker usable
threshold. This is an inference and research request, not a derived claim.
It must retain the physical output length m_seed*n and the actual terminal
leaf loss from C72.

A useful answer needs a proved phase/DP matching, a legitimate input
subsequence, a quantitative strict margin and an actual full certificate.
An unevaluated infimum involving an unknown phase is not a found seed.
Nor is a small finite gain above P_r automatically enough.

[Chikhi,arXiv2609.13765v1](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.13765v1)
was also screened. Its Theorem1's exact objective is F(z_N), where
z_N=prox_(lambda F)(x_N), in relaxed proximal point. The sharp eta/4 factor
there is not the original smooth f(x_N) objective coefficient. The paper's
smooth constant-step optimality statement concerns final gradient norm.
Neither result directly provides an improving schedule for this goal.
This is an applicability boundary, not a criticism of those results.

No new optimizer, phase scan, installation, verifier, or external publication
was made. The full-primitive44-evaluation batch is closed; its28 unused
evaluations and unused time have expired.

Context only; no Danus fact or matched theorem. Main read
[NIST DLMF17.2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/17.2) and17.5 on2026-09-27.
DLMF17.2.1 defines the finite q-Pochhammer product. Equations17.2.7--17.2.20
give finite product transformations, and17.2.35 gives the finite q-binomial
theorem. The series identity17.2.37 carries a convergence condition on z;
infinite-product conventions must also be checked. Finite polynomial
identities should be preferred when they suffice.

The published worker draft cefde310a2e5268e already has an explicit inverse
birth-coordinate formula using q_c=1/c and products
P_r=product_(s=1)^r(1-q_c^s), derived there by interpolation. This note
does NOT claim that NIST supplies the six missing coefficient inequalities.
A possible next mechanism is to use exact finite product/cancellation
identities to expose the actual row sums and signed tail terms before
estimating them. This is a research suggestion, not a derived result.

Required boundaries: retain the physical additive1 in d_j, the same
enclosing-m parameters, exact finite truncation (especially the penultimate
row), all alternating signs and the full constant load. Replacing every
signed term by an independent absolute bound may lose the needed margin.
Positivity of Gaussian binomial coefficients alone does not imply positivity
of the target quadratic form. Any infinite comparison needs uniform,
quantitative truncation bounds in BOTH row and horizon indices.

The six hypotheses in the terminal-charge package are still unproved.
Neither a source name nor a formal q-product rewriting is their proof.
No kernel replay, solver, installation, verifier or allocation change was
made by this source check.

# Primary-source scope refresh, 2026-09-30

This is a main-owned retrieval note, not a Danus fact or a proof review.
The search was bounded and does not establish absence of other literature.

## Lower bounds

Ye--Liu, [Silver Rate Is (Almost) Optimal for Gradient Descent,
v2](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.09152v2), was reopened on September30.
Theorem1.1 bounds every nonnegative finite-horizon word, but its displayed
exponent includes an additional positive term of order
sqrt(log log n/log n). Equation1 uses this project's W normalization.
Its stated result therefore does not supply the requested leading
constant or exact B_n profile. This is the same source already scoped
in SOURCE_NOTES.md, not a newly discovered target-resolution theorem.
Sections2 and3.1 identify checkpoint selection, changing directions and
combining local functions as the construction interfaces; AppendixB.3
retains compactness, zero coordinates and splitting at a tight checkpoint.
Any renewed route must overcome the actual quantitative loss with one
static function and true initial distance, rather than merely restating
the exponent theorem. This last sentence is a research requirement, not
a claimed mathematical improvement.

## Recursive benchmark

The primary abstract for Liu--Chen--Jiang--Wang,
[Optimal Recursive Composition and Dyadic Phase Laws for Gradient Descent
with Predetermined Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11788), still
describes optimization inside recursive frameworks and log-periodic
profiles. Its scope supplies no automatic identification with arbitrary
predetermined schedules. The prior direct HTML scope check in
SOURCE_NOTES.md remains the relevant warning about ambient certificate
classes versus recursively generated classes.

No source was imported into the fact graph. No new model consult,
mathematical replay, formal verifier call or worker round was opened by
this retrieval. All project resource closures remain in force.

# Methodological source scope: BnB-PEP

Primary sources inspected:
- [Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2203.07305)
- [Authors' code](https://github.com/Shuvomoy/BnB-PEP-code)

The paper jointly parameterizes optimization methods and their
performance certificates through nonconvex constrained optimization.
It distinguishes candidate construction from certified global search.
This project borrows only the joint-variable modeling idea for a small
local discovery pilot. It has not implemented the paper's global
branch-and-bound algorithm or imported a theorem from it.

Here the object is the ORIGINAL primitive h/Lambda/full-PSD certificate,
not automatically the paper's chosen performance-estimation objective.
The exact reformulation,all signs/flow/PSD conditions and the actual
seed-to-profile interface must therefore be proved in the project.
Local solver output,finite search restrictions and floating feasibility
are not exact certificates or nonexistence proofs. No package or
commercial solver installation was made by this source inspection.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001649 -->
# Primary-source tools for the current structural routes

Checked September27,2026. Main-authored source triage, not a proof, imported
fact or external consult. These optional tools do not change the assignments,
launch limits or closed numerical/verification allowances.

## Symmetry and matrix algebra

Primary source: de Klerk, Pasechnik and Schrijver, *Reduction of symmetric
semidefinite programs using the regular star-representation* (2007),
[author-hosted paper](https://homepages.cwi.nl/~lex/files/symm.pdf).
Read Section2, Theorem1 and equations17-19. The regular representation of the
commutant is a star-isomorphism, preserving positive semidefiniteness. In the
displayed SDP application, a permutation group commutes with the objective
and constraint matrices; averaging therefore preserves feasibility and cost.

Possible use for max: test whether a proposed bulk tensor coefficient family
has an actual closed matrix algebra with a smaller faithful representation.
This is a research suggestion, not a verified match. Repeated step values or
a dyadic indexing set alone do not establish the source's invariance
hypotheses for chronological GD, its optimum edges, or its last objective.
A Walsh/Haar coordinate change alone supplies no new feasible array. Keep
actual coefficient nonnegativity separate from PSD of a transformed matrix.

## Chordal PSD versus polynomial certificates

Primary source: Zheng and Fantuzzi, *Sum-of-squares chordal decomposition of
polynomial matrix inequalities*,
[full paper](https://arxiv.org/html/2007.11410).
Theorem2.1 states the classical clique-supported PSD-sum characterization for
a constant matrix with chordal sparsity. Proposition2.1 shows that even an
everywhere positive definite polynomial matrix need not have a polynomial
clique decomposition of the naive same form. Theorem2.4 obtains weighted SOS
clique decompositions on an Archimedean compact semialgebraic domain under
strict positive definiteness.

Possible use for max/max3: an explicitly structured full remainder might
admit clique-sized storage/Schur certificates. First identify its ACTUAL
matrix sparsity and retained separator variables. A graph of interpolation
edges is not automatically the sparsity graph of the full quadratic matrix.
Neither pointwise constant-matrix decomposition nor an existence theorem
supplies a uniform-degree all-depth formula. Singular PSD boundaries and a
parameter tending to zero require their own proof; strict positivity cannot
be silently supplied by a numerical check. This is conditional source triage.

## Spectral sparsification has a load-bearing PSD-atom hypothesis

Primary source: de Carli Silva, Harvey and Sato, *Sparse Sums of Positive
Semidefinite Matrices*,
[paper](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1107.0088), Theorem3 and AppendixD.
For PSD summands B_i of size d, their sum B admits a nonnegative weighted
O(d/epsilon^2)-term approximation lying between B and (1+epsilon)B. The paper
explicitly says the assumption that the summands are PSD cannot be removed.

Possible use only after an actual positive-block decomposition exists.
C55's individual added-pair update is indefinite, so this theorem does not
justify sparsifying those updates as if they were PSD atoms. Exact value-flow
and interpolation-coefficient constraints also require an explicitly matched
argument; approximate spectral preservation alone does not preserve them or
the exact target constant. No randomized or deterministic solver is allocated.

## Arbitrary spectra do not substitute for gradient legality

Primary source: Greenbaum, Ptak and Strakos, *Any Nonincreasing Convergence
Curve is Possible for GMRES* (1996),
[publisher abstract](https://epubs.siam.org/doi/abs/10.1137/S0895479894275030).
The abstract permits a prescribed positive nonincreasing residual history
with arbitrarily specified eigenvalues for a constructed linear system.
Only the publisher statement was used here; no full proof was retrieved.

This is an analogy/caution for max2's temporal-span work, not a theorem about
GD. GMRES residual minimization and a general linear system do not establish
cyclic smooth-convex interpolation, gradient integrability, or the correct
objective/initial-distance normalization. The current legal finite example
c5a096d73740bf81 already demonstrates the relevant radius-only failure within
this project. No additional GMRES detour is requested absent a concrete
legality-preserving construction.

# Future-only original source three-entry cut

UNVERIFIED MAIN ANALYTICAL DERIVATION. No mathematical program, numerical replay or formal call. This note is not a new worker allocation and is not an accepted fact. Independently rederive it after FULL reading of max93's final report before using it to close any route.

For the current architecture retain h0=h2=b=1+a, h1=g=1+beta, Ltri as usual, P=K+v v^T+eta z z^T, N=Ltri^T(P+A), A skew, Ae=F(v-z), Ev+Fz=Pe, E=pi+s, F=pi-s, eta=F/E and q=pi^2. Assume alpha0=0, v0=bs/a, z0=bE/a, e^Tz=E, e^Tv=E, and Ke=0. These hold for the literal max93 common column; preserving z1 is not needed for this proposed cut.

The ORIGINAL first row of Ltri^T is b e^T-a e0^T. Consequently, for EVERY j,

 N0j=b*s*v_j+b*F*z_j-a*K0j-a*A0j,
 Pj0-A0j=2*Kj0+N0j/a.

The latter also holds at j=0: P00=K00+b^2*q/a^2 and N00=b^2*q/a-a*K00. Thus the COMPLETE source column satisfies the exact identity

 N_:0=2*Ltri^T*K_:0+(Ltri^T*N_0:^T)/a.

Since mu0=2bq, put r0=N00-mu0=(1/a-a)q-a*K00. At original row2, multiplication gives

 a*N20=2a*(Ltri^T K)_20+N02+b*sum_(j>2)N0j.

Using sum_(j>0)N0j=-r0 and b=1+a yields the THREE ORIGINAL ENTRY identity

 a*N20+b*N01+a*N02=2a*(Ltri^T K)_20-b*r0.          (S3)

All three weights are positive and all three entries are off diagonal. If the right side is positive, no compatible skew can make every original finite multiplier nonnegative. This is stronger than a failure of a sparse particular completion, but only for the specified source column of K and the specified common-source normalization.

For the currently frozen metric, the only source-reaching detail is d0=(g,-b-g,b,0,...). All remaining details, including the terminal-six Rtest block, have zero source coordinate. Let rho=rho_clock and Kclock=rho*(diag(h)-h h^T/S). Since K00=3q and omega0=(3q-Kclock00)/g^2,

 (Ltri^T K)_20
 =3bq/g-rho*b^2/g*(1-b/S)
          -rho*b^2*[1+(1-2b-g)/S]
 >3bq/g-rho*b^2*(1+1/g).

The established scalar enclosures for this architecture are a>2/5, a<1/2, b>7/5, b<3/2, 1<g<2, rho<125/S^2, q>1/(3S), S>2^20. With r0=(1/a-4a)q, the proposed bound from(S3) is

 2a*(Ltri^T K)_20-b*r0
 >b*(7a-1/a)*q-5rho
 >(21/50)q-625/S^2
 >(41/100)q > (2/5)q.

Here 7a-1/a increases for positive a and exceeds3/10 at a>2/5. Also 625/S^2<(1875/S)q<q/100 because S>2^20>187500. These comparisons are textual derivations, not executed arithmetic.

If this identity and bounds survive independent checking, they exclude the ENTIRE max93 fixed-metric completion, including arbitrary global skew, despite its repaired global prefix/clock tests. They do not exclude a changed source-reaching metric or changed common source coordinate. In particular adding only an omitted0-to4 skew coupling cannot repair a positive(S3) invariant. A source metric that touches earlier coordinates in a new way or a non-optimal z0 would need all moments, charge, source/clock conditions and the full PSD rederived; no such repair is constructed here.

Important provenance correction: the max93 TASK's suggested expanded Csrc numerator contained a sign typo. The literal definition uses p1*w1=+g*a^2/(1+s), as the worker reported, and the frozen common-column law itself was unchanged. This source-cut derivation uses only z0 and does not use either expansion of Csrc. Do not cite the erroneous minus-sign expansion in a later proof.

Original minimax target OPEN. This is a future candidate obstruction to a specified certificate architecture, not a primal function or a lower/upper minimax theorem.

# Tree embedding as a possible reserve-payment tool

Primary-source method lookup, October8. Contextual lead only; no imported Danus fact, matched GD theorem, mathematical program or verification request.

Arcozzi, Holmes, Mozolyako and Volberg, [Bellman Function Sitting on a Tree](https://academic.oup.com/imrn/article/2021/16/12037/5610523), Section1.1, Theorem1.3, gives a Carleson embedding on a dyadic tree with an explicit factor4. In finite-tree notation, let mu_v>=0 and let T_v be the descendants of v including v. A rescaling of the stated condition is

    sum_(v in T_u) t_v [mu(T_v)]^2 <= C mu(T_u),  for every u,

with t_v>=0. The conclusion is

    sum_v t_v [sum_(w in T_v) mu_w y_w]^2 <= 4 C sum_w mu_w y_w^2.

The paper proves its tree inequality using the Bellman majorant4(F-f^2/(v+A)); it also distinguishes the tree setting from the usual martingale setting. The relevant displayed hypotheses and Section1 proof setup were read. This source is an existing theorem, not a newly established research result.

## Proposed use and missing identification

The extension to finitely many Hilbert coordinates follows by applying the scalar inequality to each coordinate and summing. That extension is a direct inference, not a separately quoted theorem.

A possible application would identify the negative nested primitive terms as averages of ACTUAL auxiliary-edge differences, and positive bridge/radial squares as the right-hand energy. The exact new radial identity is in main_radial_auxiliary_reserve_20261008_r3.md. Any application must exhibit the actual mu,t, every physical/auxiliary input, all cross terms, the boundary conditions and a uniform finite-m bound on C. Its factor4 must fit the available target margin. No such identification or constant comparison has been obtained.

The completed right-suffix and initial-only chains have a zero-aggregate negative direction which all their existing reserves miss. Applying a generic embedding theorem to those same coordinates cannot repair that null-direction failure. New internal-block coordinates or changed original-row couplings are required before this source could be relevant. This is a reopening condition, not a new proof allocation.

The earlier SOURCE_HARDY_KERNEL_20260927.md already discussed one-dimensional weighted Hardy bounds and their factor losses. The present lookup concerns an actual branching-tree theorem; neither note proves a constant-preserving GD comparison. No search for a posted solution of the exact benchmark was made.

<!-- END_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001651 -->

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001652 -->
# Source lead: coefficient signs and dual arc diagrams

Primary sources checked September27,2026. This note is awareness/strategy,
not a verifier-accepted theorem for the project.

## General certificate congruence still needs signs

Shu--Wang, [A Unified Theory of H-Duality in First-Order Methods](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.03281v1), Sections1.1--1.2,4.3--4.7 and AppendixC.

The paper transforms certificate matrices so that the associated full slack
forms are congruent. The remaining coefficient-sign conditions are explicit;
PSD preservation alone is insufficient. Proposition2 supplies sign preservation
for invertible reduced tridiagonal certificates in the listed D/G performance
pairings. The main text also points to a combinatorial characterization in the
source below. This does not establish reversal of an arbitrary intrinsic V_h
certificate: its performance setup, actual signs and any certificate selection
must still be matched. The main agent separately confirmed the scope audit
already published as4ad2db6604a59a2c.

## A precise combinatorial sign criterion in another setting

Yoon--Grimmer, [A Theory of Composition and Duality of Extremal Optimal Fixed-Point Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/html/2605.02231v2), v2 August6,2026, Sections3--4, especially Theorem4 and its proof in4.2.2.

The setting is minimax-optimal vertex algorithms for nonexpansive fixed-point
residuals. Their interpolation-multiplier supports are encoded by arc diagrams.
Theorem4 says the antitransposed vertex method remains optimal exactly when
its arc diagram is noncrossing; the resulting method is again a vertex. The
proof relates signs of finite differences of an inverse certificate matrix to
weighted graph/forest structure. Crossing examples in this class show why a
generic appeal to time reversal is insufficient. The theorem is not about
arbitrary predetermined GD schedules or the intrinsic primitive energy.

## Concrete applicability test, not an imported conclusion

For max4's proposed general reversal interface, the possible lead is to seek
a sign-controlling structure in the ACTUAL intrinsic certificate array, or an
exact obstruction to a proposed matrix transform. First match the performance
functional, row/column constraints, reduction/invertibility and the distinction
between a given certificate and all certificates for the schedule. Failure of
one transformed certificate does not disprove existence of another certificate.
An optimal-vertex assumption cannot be imposed on the current problem without
proof. Likewise, no noncrossing or decomposition hypothesis may be presumed.

These are DUAL multiplier graphs. C35/C39's primal coordinate-flow graph
obstructions do not automatically exclude this proof architecture. Conversely,
the source's vertex classification does not validate those primal constructions.
This is an optional mechanism lead inside the current task, with no additional
numerical, verification, model or round allocation.

<!-- END_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001652 -->

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001653 -->
# Two scoped primary-source leads

Read on September27,2026. These are literature leads, not accepted Danus facts.
The original exact all-horizon objective target is unchanged.

## Controlled repair and inherited-component interference

Liu--Ye, [Silver Rate Is (Almost) Optimal for Gradient Descent: The Strongly Convex Case](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.26873v1), v1 September22,2026.

Source scope: Theorem1.1 and Corollary1.2 concern strong convexity, relative
distance/function reduction and a subpolynomial condition-number loss. They do
not prove the convex exact constant/profile target. Sections2.2,3.2--3.3,4.2
and5.2 track activation level relative to remaining amplitude. The repair
construction controls this ratio; Lemma3.3 inserts a finite triangle-envelope
bridge that tolerates a nonnegative contribution from the previous component,
then removes interference in the next component's input coordinate. Complete
inactivity of an older component is not required. The stated bridge assumes
kappa>=4, small gap mass relative to kappa, positive available amplitude and
checkpoint size greater than4. Its transfer pays an explicit constant loss.

Main inference to test, not a theorem from this paper: the geometric lane may
benefit from tolerating a controlled inherited gradient rather than demanding
zero contribution everywhere. First compare this with the actual retained
Phi/Psi/Dist/G/Xi interface. A direct citation cannot remove the constant loss
or import a strong-convex limit into the current convex family. If the number
of repairs grows or the loss already defeats the near-critical scale, record
that exact failure and do not rebuild the exponent proof.

## Graph-structured full slack certificates

Bok--Altschuler, [Accelerating Proximal Gradient Descent via Silver Stepsizes](https://optimization-online.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/12/proxgd_stepsize_accel.pdf).

Source scope: Sections1.2 and3, Theorem3.1, Definition3.5 and Lemma3.6 concern
the specified silver proximal-GD construction. Two sets of nonnegative
interpolation multipliers and a full slack matrix certify multi-step descent.
After taking a Schur complement, the relevant matrix has Laplacian structure;
nonpositive off-diagonal entries and zero row sums give its PSD certificate.
The construction is recursive and does not establish completeness for arbitrary
GD certificates, general signed-terminal-credit re-selection, or minimax
optimality of arbitrary schedules. Its sign convention for interpolation is
opposite to this project's Q<=0 convention.

Main inference to test, not a result of the source: max4's re-selection problem
could be screened for an actual graph/Schur-complement repair which preserves
value flow and every coefficient of the full gradient matrix. A PSD graph
identity alone does not repair negative coefficients on actual interpolation
inequalities. The existing tight intrinsic-memory rigidity still applies to
its stated class; merely adding positive quadratic memory is not a new route.

## Dispatch boundary

These leads supplement the current concrete tasks; they do not replace the
near-critical diagnostic, renew solver/verification allowances, extend round
caps or authorize new model types. Use the sources only after checking their
actual hypotheses, and publish the result of the applicability test in shared
evidence. No external theorem has been added to the fact graph.

<!-- END_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001653 -->

# Exact-profile minimax research checkpoint

Project gd-exact-profile-20260926. Shared index read through UTC2026-09-26T15:50:04.343712Z (Shanghai23:50); refresh live state before action. This main-owned continuation map is not a truth store. Earlier21:17 checkpoint: intake CHECKPOINT_ARCHIVE_20260926_2117.md.

## Fixed goal

UNRESOLVED. GD has finite predetermined nonnegative steps h, x_(t+1)=x_t-h_t grad f(x_t)/L. W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_*)/(L||x_0-x_*||^2), over every finite dimension and convex L-smooth f with a minimizer. W_n^*=inf_h W_n(h). The target is W_n^*/U_n^ZJ ->1, uniformly in the precise sense: every epsilon>0 admits N_epsilon independent of h such that W_n(h)>=(1-epsilon)U_n^ZJ for ALL n>=N_epsilon and ALL h>=0. A negative answer needs a fixed relative gain on unbounded horizons with full-class upper certificates.

The exact benchmark is U_n^ZJ=1/B_n. Set P_0=B_0=1,
F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2,
P_n=max_(k+l=n-1)F(P_k,P_l), B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(P_k,B_l).
rho=log_2(1+sqrt2). Exponent-only, finite-horizon, scalar-only, restricted-schedule or numerical results do not complete the Goal. Neither0.4239 nor an assumed normalized limit replaces B_n.

Goal01a0dcdf-084a-7331-8332-56716b98d77a is active. PROBLEM.md remains the exact supplied source, SHA256 3ECC3159A696E8528EBC727776D42F657CFBC6842E9212B28C870FCD05EDB89D. Attachment, intake SOURCE_PROBLEM.md and PROBLEM.md were previously freshly matched. W=2R is essential. SOURCE_NOTES.md retains literature and normalization checks.

## Six accepted facts

| Fact | Accepted role | Boundary |
|---|---|---|
| 0be8b54e0a33381c | Universal Huber bound; finite terminal-library obstruction under unchanged ConPD and stated prefix caps; square-root transport | Not the unrestricted converse |
| 0e7e78056dc6258c | Pairwise-orthogonal-gradient moment obstruction | Correlated geometry remains open |
| b98bb807d514e80d | Exact asymmetric single-cut witnesses and their insufficiency on a stated family | Not all nonlinear functions |

## Goal and fixed benchmark

ACTIVE/UNRESOLVED. GD uses a finite predetermined h in[0,infinity)^n:
x_(t+1)=x_t-h_t grad f(x_t)/L. W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_*)/(L||x_0-x_*||^2),
over every finite dimension, convex L-smooth f with a minimizer and x_0!=x_*.
W_n^*=inf_h W_n(h). The target is W_n^*/U_n^ZJ ->1, or a certified fixed
asymptotic relative improvement disproving it. For the affirmative direction,
every epsilon>0 needs N_epsilon independent of h, with W_n(h)>=(1-epsilon)/B_n
for ALL n>=N_epsilon and ALL h>=0. A negative answer needs unbounded horizons,
one fixed delta>0 and full-class upper certificates W_n(h)<=(1-delta)/B_n.

Exact comparator: P_0=B_0=1,
F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2,
P_n=max_(k+l=n-1)F(P_k,P_l), B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(P_k,B_l), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.
rho=log_2(1+sqrt2). Keep W=2R. No decimal0.4239, assumed profile limit,
exponent-only result, finite case, scalar-only theorem, numerical candidate or
restricted schedule theorem counts as completion.

# Exact-profile minimax research checkpoint

Project: gd-exact-profile-20260926. This is a main-owned continuation map,
NOT a Danus truth store. Updated after live receipts at

## Fixed target and evidence boundary

Goal ACTIVE; original problem UNRESOLVED. The process is an actual Danus
deployment, with Codex CLI as its worker/verifier model backend. The desktop
session is the main orchestrator. Consult is off; main strategy is self-authored.

For finite predetermined h in [0,infinity)^n,
x_(t+1)=x_t-h_t grad f(x_t)/L, and
W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x0-x_star||^2), over every finite dimension
and convex L-smooth function with a minimizer, x0!=x_star. W_n^*=inf_h W_n(h).
Prove W_n^*/U_n^ZJ ->1 with an epsilon threshold independent of h, for EVERY
sufficiently large n and EVERY nonnegative h, or disprove it by a certified
fixed relative improvement along unbounded horizons. W=2R.

Exact definitions: P0=B0=1,
F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2,
P_n=max_(k+l=n-1)F(P_k,P_l),
B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(P_k,B_l), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.
lambda=1+sqrt2 and rho=log_2 lambda. No decimal profile constant, exponent-only
bound, restricted method, finite case or numerical result resolves the goal.
The adversary may depend on the entire predetermined schedule/horizon, but
must remain one legal function during its trajectory.

## Current mathematical frontier

### C54 removes the coarse scalar-comparison bottleneck

Complete draft e3c8501149a5c47e proves
G(x,lambda y)<=lambda F(x,y), B_n<=lambda P_n,
P_n<=(n+1)^rho and P_(2^m-1)=lambda^m. It does not assume a profile limit.
Main read the full elementary proof. Mirror SHA256:
27bdcecd4e32a126f4f10057aad88bee926ef2335f77ea01110b7a9210914bc7.

The actual critical family is
c_m=lambda(1+1/m), a_m=(1-1/m)/c_m,
H0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_m c_m^j,H_j), j<m,
n_m=2^m-1, M_m=1+sum H_m. Parameters are fixed for the entire horizon.
The draft proves M_m/lambda^m->e, and with K_Hub=1/(2M_m-1),
limsup B_(n_m) K_Hub<=lambda/(2e).
Thus a genuine full upper q_m<=1/M_m is SUFFICIENT for a negative resolution,
since limsup q_m B_(n_m)<=lambda/e<1. More generally
limsup q_m M_m<e/lambda suffices. This replaces the old coarse2/e threshold;
it does not prove any full upper certificate exists. C54 must be included
and checked in an eventual complete target package.

### Full objective construction remains missing

C51 047c257795dc59ba gives an exact draft full9x9 certificate for H3 at
q=9/(139+52sqrt2)=Huber. Its general quotient parameterization is lossless
ONLY at Huber. Its nullspace and zero optimum-outgoing coefficients must not
be imposed as necessary on q>Huber. Main read its statement/chart; exact
derivations are public evidence. The finite result is not an asymptotic proof.

C53 bc018be5d1eaecd6 excludes the specified whole mixed repeated-copy join,
not all certificates. 1f81b116bd1128ab extends this to seed depth r_m if
2^(m-r_m)/m^2->infinity. The formal bad template violates an actual queried
pair (n-4,n-1), so it cannot be a legal lower witness. Adding both directions
of that pair repairs its two-variable restriction but is indefinite on the
full space. C55 supplies an attained three-boundary H2 seed and explicit
six-variable join with exact Schur/range obligations. Its all-depth feasible
parameters and final v_m>=M_m remain unknown. This is max3's current route.

max's complete draft314dc8801a8e7019 also excludes the relaxed D=M physical-
weight four-endpoint-fan family for every q at m>=128, even with the stated
signed child corrections and nonsaturating remainders. Earlier independent-
history and AP arrays also fail within their stated assumptions. The next
max route leaves their support/weight restrictions simultaneously via a
bulk full-array organization; a mere basis change is not a new result.

### Lower constructions and queried cuts remain independent

cebf601250c82dc2 completes the fixed two-complex-mode bound for ALL normalized
common PSD metrics, including initial correlations. Retuning amplitudes or
adding rows inside that temporal span is no longer a new route. Its claim
is not an all-normal/all-nonnormal/all-function theorem.

c5a096d73740bf81 constructs a legal finite schedule(10,1) example with three
tiny real modes and an SPD common metric, refuting a radius-only Huber cap.
Its n+1-node representation covers all legal prefixes and therefore just
reparameterizes the original problem. max2 now seeks a genuinely structured
growing temporal span and complete all-pair legality, not an unrestricted
metric restatement. A weak quadratic in disguise is only a calibration.

8d60e800f95e6026 gives explicit arbitrary-q terminal coefficient redistribution,
with full PSD and optimizer edges retained. 4cf747fea7fe2e00 proves every
positive queried step must retain incident interpolation edges in any such
certificate. Earlier vertices cannot be deleted by iterating the terminal
operation. max4 now seeks quantitative cut costs and an explicit closing
boundary state, rather than another qualitative incidence statement.

Earlier exact draft exclusions remain scoped: C_spine repair needs its
saturated-tail/short-leaf decomposition; the adjacent criterion explicitly
forces h<2; intrinsic reversal needs inverse off-diagonal signs; zero full
remainder is solved but nonzero remainder is not; scalar ray, fixed spectral,

Their detailed IDs/statements remain in ROUTE_REGISTRY.md and archived
checkpoints. Old c=13/5 words without the +1 do not automatically transfer

## Exact target and fixed definitions

For every finite predetermined h in[0,infinity)^n,
x_(t+1)=x_t-h_t grad f(x_t)/L and
W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x0-x_star||^2), over every finite dimension,
convex L-smooth f with a minimizer and x0!=x_star. W_n^*=inf_h W_n(h).
The target is W_n^*/U_n^ZJ ->1, or a rigorous asymptotic disproof.

P0=B0=1,
F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,

finite-horizon, restricted-schedule, numerical, or conditional results do

## Current mathematical routes and exact blockers

### 1. Exact benchmark rather than a single critical candidate

max's latest task starts from an ACTUAL Zhang-Jiang dominant word and full
certificate at1/B_n, with deterministic exact maximizing splits, then varies
both prescribed steps and the complete array. It must achieve a fixed relative
gain at unbounded horizons with a uniform finite admissible radius. A local
derivative, small-horizon improvement or comparison only to Silver is not
enough. Accepted0be8b54e0a33381c excludes merely changing a fixed finite
terminal library under unchanged ConPD/prefix caps; any successful variation
must leave those hypotheses explicitly. Plans7628a8e6fc7f96ab and
fbb8565d7b4cf075 name the actual source recovery and global variation tests.

Previous full Silver continuation is jointly recorded in1f135237f7524f69 and
e8ab1de28a654fb0. It reconstructs both the actual AP full baseline and a
separate sharp full array q=1/(2M-1). Its dense rank-one transport has a
zero-multiplier sign failure even after the stated skew repairs. Rank-one
mass-rate continuation requires unbounded relative derivatives. A genuinely
full higher-rank analytic repair is feasible, but its displayed q diverges.
These are scoped failures, not a universal affordable-continuation no-go.
Public mirror e8ab1de28a654fb0.md SHA256
ae3616b4cf44051b51086801fdb3ba7a620a5f8827c375be848ea4481c542648.

### 2. Joint positive heights and common Gram budget

max2 keeps the restricted growing quotient chi=z^2 product_j(z-1/d_j),
with d_j the ACTUAL physical ruler steps, common vectors u,v,w_j, and
R_i=sum_j P_i(1/d_j)w_j, Z_i=sum_j d_j P_i(1/d_j)w_j.
All values/heights are shared, all cross-mode terms remain, and
||x0||=||u+Z0||=1. No mode is required to be individually legal.

Draft468f3abd12846a0f closes the zero-height tilted-projection realization.
28db18b9be162b29 closes ONE anisotropic metric-normal height policy with
condition number<=2, not arbitrary Q-only heights. Actual examples
456e36fd0eb02833 refute automatic normals N<=0 from Q<=0 and transfer of a
highest-mode Rayleigh bound to every full gradient.

For the Q-only common quadratic-height policy, ac92ae33abcc02c4 gives only
a highest-mode lower cost d_j-17. It does not sum across modes or bound
the common initial distance. The exact shared budget30e88f2351b2aa4f is
eta>=delta0+||R0||^2+<v,R0>-<R0,x0>. It yields the stated terminal inequalities
but still needs a JOINT charge controlling negative <v,Z0>. The current
task seeks that budget or a useful legal construction without extra normal,
bounded-conditioning or independent-value assumptions. Nested covariance,
Schur or ordered-kernel methods are proposals, not imported theorems.

Earlier growing reciprocal/simple-zero, near-unit Jordan, and specified
level-perturbed classes are excluded only in5f198236d1ff200e/a4b68ba03decaca2.
f1a908ff0d086d78's double-zero counterexample is ACTUALLY legal but exactly
the known rank-one first-cut payoff, not a stronger adversary. It also
refutes a tempting nonnegative cross-term shortcut.

### 3. Proper increment cone and signed interval construction

Draft e01c11b179127b30 strengthens869f567eefca9076 to EVERY h>=0, with
explicit original-space zero-step lifting and both attainments. The common
array's tests on all chronological three-block gradient patterns equal a
legal interpolation problem with a gradient-INCREMENT Gram that is a sum
of PSD blocks on pairs. It is a proper cone, not unrestricted full PEP.
Its value at1/M_m and its exact all-h profile comparison remain unknown.

In common jump coordinates (x0,g0,d_1,...,d_n), the tests involve one base
block C, range conditions for all columns, and every2x2 principal block of
the residual Z. This does NOT imply Z is PSD. Independent per-cut arrays,
dropped position moments and direct nonnegative Schur closure are invalid.
At q=1/M the necessary optimum-source budget is
sum_i(S-P_i)nu_i+(M/4)(sum nu_i)^2<=S; it does not force nu_i=0.

max4's new signed interval mixture is a genuinely correlated primal lane.
The specific zero-height reflection/extinction rule e09fd8046ee1daf2 has
payoff<3/M_m^2 and is closed at all dyadic cutoffs. Its positive-height
extension is NOT closed: exact Huber example690761f5e926551d refutes that
extension of the bound. The broader interval cone remains open.

The saved H5 numerical object's cuts4,8,12 are incompatible with the two-
jump cone in ff4d6dbf068fc6a8, corrected by59a18c56c5f0af76 (exact saved-
decimal witness -0.03934212331225688964). This is a saved-data compatibility
statement, not an exact function or a bound on W(H5). C59's separate exact
trajectory is outside even six-jump representations. Neither fact establishes
that every two-jump construction is weak; do not force those data into it.

### 4. Closed repair classes and critical scalar comparison

C56 ab39085b21b02377 constructs a legal R2 repair of its fixed C55 scalar
data by one monotone quadratic coordinate, but even free maximal terminal
completion has limsup M*payoff<=1/e. Erratum5449b0b644f2a9f3 changes the
summary's necessary T^2 inequality from > to >=; full proof already had >=.
C57 816b673b5988f6c3 enlarges to arbitrary-dimensional ordered/acute-cone
auxiliary repairs, with sharp leading M*payoff=1/2 under its stated minimizer/
distance conditions. Turning/signed directions and changed scalar data are
outside both theorems. Do not reopen another ordered variant.

C53/C55 prescribed seed/join families are excluded in their exact scopes;
arbitrary seeds/internal corrections/full arrays remain open. Normalized
dense tensor-mixture arrays273cc121acfd549e force q>1/8 even on undeformed
Silver under their stated hypotheses; dense support alone did not remove
their first/terminal coefficient coupling. These are not legal lower witnesses.

For reference, the critical family is c_m=lambda(1+1/m),
a_m=(1-1/m)/c_m, H0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_m c_m^j,H_j), M_m=1+sum H_m.
C54 draft e3c8501149a5c47e proves B_n<=lambda P_n,
P_n<=(n+1)^rho, P_(2^m-1)=lambda^m and M_m/lambda^m->e.
Thus limsup q_m M_m<e/lambda, in particular q_m<=1/M_m eventually,
would SUFFICE for a negative resolution IF a full upper certificate existed.
It does not exist merely from scalar mass or finite Huber agreement. C59
only excludes the finite m=6 bound, not an eventual large-m claim.

## Fixed goal and evidence boundary

UNRESOLVED. Prove W_n^*/U_n^ZJ -> 1 for the exact supplied horizon-dependent benchmark, uniformly over every nonnegative predetermined schedule and all sufficiently large horizons, or rigorously disprove it by a fixed relative improvement on unbounded horizons with full-class upper certificates. W=2(f_n-f_*)/(L||x_0-x_*||^2), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n, and B_n=2S_bullet(n)+1. The persistent Goal in task 01a0dcdf-084a-7331-8332-56716b98d77a is active. Exponent-only, finite-horizon, scalar-only, restricted-schedule and numerical results do not complete it.

The exact original PROBLEM.md is unchanged. Its recorded SHA-256 is 3ECC3159A696E8528EBC727776D42F657CFBC6842E9212B28C870FCD05EDB89D. The source attachment and intake SOURCE_PROBLEM.md were copied without modification. SOURCE_NOTES.md retains the normalization and literature scope checks.

It defines the exact shifted F/G recurrences, the six closed components, current conditional proof skeleton and theorem-strength gaps. Latest coordinated guidance: 8d2dacb475d6eb19.
