### Geometric lane

The two-cell construction ce636998be58c115 supplies actual nonperiodic ordinary-block inputs, exact cross-plane inequalities and an actual minimum-distance formula. Its increasing inherited planes prove that compression to the last cell is invalid. The follow-up state test 97e8798c6b1e9dfc gives finite exact updates of Phi (old-plane envelope), Psi (old-query acceptance envelope) and Dist (distance to the true zero set in an outgoing slice), provided the actual boundary gradient magnitude G is retained. Qualification 6348179e97b86202 gives two legal histories with identical three profiles and different G, hence different next points. Fresh coordinates must be orthogonal to the entire old ambient, not merely old normals.

The complete three-cell example is in research/max_three_profile_state.md. Candidate bd45ea19cf2aeaf6 gives an explicit necessary level/scale budget for positive arc chains. An empty zero slice cannot be repaired by silently changing the minimum while retaining the old Dist function. The missing piece is a constructive policy and uniform payoff for arbitrary schedules. The function-valued recurrence alone is not the target theorem.

Earlier df2a5653a864f938 provides affine guards and bounded backward propagation after a specified periodic prefix, resisting a linear-length arbitrary suffix. Its prefix hypothesis remains essential. The function may depend on the whole prescribed schedule, so robustness of one fixed function to every suffix is never imposed as a general requirement.

### Complete-certificate lane

The new accepted terminal component is now available. The next plan 6bbf6bbda0aca42f concerns the exact convex-hull terminal potential and optimization order. No unproved exchange between schedule minimization and function maximization is allowed, and simplex certificate weights do not turn GD into an adaptive method.

Draft max2_global_forward_face.md (81a9c03cf73670f3) supplies a unique global two-step optimum with a unique full certificate and a strictly positive ADJACENT forward multiplier. It excludes universal triangular re-selection, even with a full stationary primal and strict complementarity. It does not exclude removing only nonadjacent remote forward edges. The previous complete remote-kernel counterexample cfe9f969be95cc9a and continuous repair 975866a657152425 remain scoped examples, not global-minimizer counterexamples.

Draft max2_whole_optimal_face.md (56f99268b45fbce8) gives whole-set source/slack rigidity and a common strict inactive-edge barrier under an actual matching stationary instance. Re-selection does not erase its costs, and earlier-cut stationarity need not survive arbitrary re-selection. Huber tightness and the subsequent exact/asymptotic DP mass restriction remain separate unresolved statements.

### Scalar lane

The exact interval framework keeps one globally fixed derivative and its full areas. The two-cut inner-return test 84abbf940857fa8d refutes the actual eleven-step primitive seed by V=-508/225. Its extension 2b63ea480303fd7c refutes the exact four-copy bridges 1+(7/10)A,1+A,1+(1/5)A for EVERY finite nonnegative seed of shifted sum A. This is not a rejection of all other joins or every direct objective certificate.

The corresponding objective channel is too weak on H_m (f2899f24e4d5962d). A stronger draft 4a53ae21f8dddf30 excludes arbitrary nested inward returns, including asymmetry, varying curvature and nonconstant ordinary gradients. Subsequent reported obstructions cover specified geometric-grid and log-periodic classes. The latest 8f56dbb73920cbe5 covers all EVEN profiles satisfying g((13/5)R)=(13/5)g(R), allowing arbitrary within-period shape. It is not an all-scalar theorem. Mixed/nonperiodic/asymmetric geometry remains open. Detailed newer scalar proofs should be read before relying on any precise extension beyond these stated scopes.

### Upper-certificate lane

Draft b750ce63e2821fa8 recovers both F and G and retains actual correlated storage from the pair (3/2,3/2). Candidate 3c02ccd1fb137184 realizes a finite residual and a PSD transport, but its unchanged scalar output mass is strictly below G. Retuning that rule cannot create a gain.

Draft max4_tight_benchmark_storage_obstruction.md (7e5ba92b7db8211a; extended by 364ad0d46c4224cb) uses four explicit equality modes to exclude all nonzero PSD intrinsic-energy remainders in the complete gradient vector at the tight three-step benchmark; it also derives uniqueness within that intrinsic class. This is not uniqueness of every full PEP certificate. A signed quantity survives, with the CORRECT identity T=I_31-I_21-I_32. The worker self-audit 4814dfa03c40344a records that sign; the absolute bound and earlier rigidity remain unchanged.

The weighted proximal-point and function-value moments cannot be dropped without a correctly signed bound. # Exact-profile minimax research checkpoint

Project gd-exact-profile-20260926. Shared index read through UTC2026-09-26T16:05:14.301922Z (Shanghai September27 00:05); the final addendum records newer dispatch receipts. ## Fixed goal

UNRESOLVED. GD has finite predetermined nonnegative steps h, x_(t+1)=x_t-h_t grad f(x_t)/L. W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_*)/(L||x_0-x_*||^2), over every finite dimension and convex L-smooth f with a minimizer. W_n^*=inf_h W_n(h). The target is W_n^*/U_n^ZJ ->1, uniformly in the precise sense: every epsilon>0 admits N_epsilon independent of h such that W_n(h)>=(1-epsilon)U_n^ZJ for ALL n>=N_epsilon and ALL h>=0. A negative answer needs a fixed relative gain on unbounded horizons with full-class upper certificates.

The exact benchmark is U_n^ZJ=1/B_n. Set P_0=B_0=1,
F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2,
P_n=max_(k+l=n-1)F(P_k,P_l), B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(P_k,B_l).
rho=log_2(1+sqrt2). Exponent-only, finite-horizon, scalar-only, restricted-schedule or numerical results do not complete the Goal. Neither0.4239 nor an assumed normalized limit replaces B_n.

Goal01a0dcdf-084a-7331-8332-56716b98d77a is active. PROBLEM.md remains the exact supplied source, SHA256 3ECC3159A696E8528EBC727776D42F657CFBC6842E9212B28C870FCD05EDB89D. ## Goal and fixed benchmark

ACTIVE/UNRESOLVED. GD uses a finite predetermined h in[0,infinity)^n:
x_(t+1)=x_t-h_t grad f(x_t)/L. W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_*)/(L||x_0-x_*||^2),
over every finite dimension, convex L-smooth f with a minimizer and x_0!=x_*.
W_n^*=inf_h W_n(h). The target is W_n^*/U_n^ZJ ->1, or a certified fixed
asymptotic relative improvement disproving it. For the affirmative direction,
every epsilon>0 needs N_epsilon independent of h, with W_n(h)>=(1-epsilon)/B_n
for ALL n>=N_epsilon and ALL h>=0. A negative answer needs unbounded horizons,
one fixed delta>0 and full-class upper certificates W_n(h)<=(1-delta)/B_n.

Exact comparator: P_0=B_0=1,
F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2,
P_n=max_(k+l=n-1)F(P_k,P_l), B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(P_k,B_l), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.
rho=log_2(1+sqrt2). Keep W=2R. No decimal0.4239, assumed profile limit,
exponent-only result, finite case, scalar-only theorem, numerical candidate or
restricted schedule theorem counts as completion.

session is the main orchestrator. Consult is off; main strategy is self-authored.

For finite predetermined h in [0,infinity)^n,
x_(t+1)=x_t-h_t grad f(x_t)/L, and
W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x0-x_star||^2), over every finite dimension
and convex L-smooth function with a minimizer, x0!=x_star. W_n^*=inf_h W_n(h).
Prove W_n^*/U_n^ZJ ->1 with an epsilon threshold independent of h, for EVERY
sufficiently large n and EVERY nonnegative h, or disprove it by a certified
fixed relative improvement along unbounded horizons. W=2R.

Exact definitions: P0=B0=1,
F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2,
P_n=max_(k+l=n-1)F(P_k,P_l),
B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(P_k,B_l), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.
lambda=1+sqrt2 and rho=log_2 lambda. No decimal profile constant, exponent-only
bound, restricted method, finite case or numerical result resolves the goal.
The adversary may depend on the entire predetermined schedule/horizon, but
must remain one legal function during its trajectory.

### C54 removes the coarse scalar-comparison bottleneck

Complete draft e3c8501149a5c47e proves
G(x,lambda y)<=lambda F(x,y), B_n<=lambda P_n,
P_n<=(n+1)^rho and P_(2^m-1)=lambda^m. It does not assume a profile limit.

27bdcecd4e32a126f4f10057aad88bee926ef2335f77ea01110b7a9210914bc7.

The actual critical family is
c_m=lambda(1+1/m), a_m=(1-1/m)/c_m,
H0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_m c_m^j,H_j), j<m,
n_m=2^m-1, M_m=1+sum H_m. Parameters are fixed for the entire horizon.
The draft proves M_m/lambda^m->e, and with K_Hub=1/(2M_m-1),
limsup B_(n_m) K_Hub<=lambda/(2e).
Thus a genuine full upper q_m<=1/M_m is SUFFICIENT for a negative resolution,
since limsup q_m B_(n_m)<=lambda/e<1. More generally
limsup q_m M_m<e/lambda suffices. This replaces the old coarse2/e threshold;
it does not prove any full upper certificate exists. C54 must be included
and checked in an eventual complete target package.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.**

The fixed goal remains lim_{n->infinity} W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1 or a rigorous disproof. Here W is the normalized last-iterate objective error for arbitrary predetermined nonnegative GD steps, and U_n^ZJ=(2S_bullet(n)+1)^(-1) uses the exact attached recursions.

Two restricted mechanisms now have signed obstructions. No accepted fact gives the missing uniform lower bound or an improved asymptotic upper bound. No leading viable proof architecture has been established. The evidence is method failure, not proposition failure. Partial objects below must not be read as near-completion.

| Item | State |
|---|---|
| Fixed goal | UNCHANGED: exact all-horizon profile comparison |
| Verified complete proof | NO |
| Verified counterexample to (P) | NO |
| Signed-closed sub-tasks | Two restricted obstruction theorems |
| Main blocker | No quantitative legal adversary covering all schedules at the exact profile; no certified asymptotically better family |
| Routes marked false/obsolete | Orthogonal-moment target-scale adversaries and fixed terminal-seed amplification, under their exact hypotheses |
| Highest-priority task | P0: obtain a concrete construction or upper-bound invariant with a quantitative interface beyond a reformulation |

| Sub-task | Status | Closed facts | Conditional facts | Missing interface |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Orthogonal-moment adversary exclusion | CLOSED | 0e7e78056dc6258c | None | None within the stated class |
| Correlated-gradient / polytope adversary | PARTIAL | None | None accepted | All-history projection inequalities and exact scale |
| Prefix-polynomial trajectory | PARTIAL | None | None accepted | Schedule-dependent cycle geometry with a sharp bound |
| Chronological envelope state | PARTIAL | None | None accepted | A quantitative invariant or lossless maximized-value comparison |
| Fixed finite terminal seed exclusion | CLOSED | 0be8b54e0a33381c | None | None within the specified ConPD class |
| Improved primitive or richer upper-bound potential | PARTIAL | None | None accepted | A gain that survives recursion and beats the exact benchmark |
| Complete proof audit | UNKNOWN | None | None | No complete candidate exists |

Current best proof skeleton:
1. Keep the exact benchmark and the original normalization.
2. Construct legal correlated interpolation data, or an exactly certified improved GD family.
3. Prove a new quantitative bound for that concrete object.
4. Establish uniformity over arbitrary schedules for the affirmative route, or nonvanishing asymptotic separation for the negative route.
5. Check every zero-step, unbounded-step, dimension, and all-horizon obligation.
6. Independently attack the complete argument before final verification.

Central missing lemma: for the affirmative route, for every epsilon>0 there must be an N_epsilon independent of h with W_n(h)>=(1-epsilon)U_n^ZJ for every n>=N_epsilon and h in [0,infinity)^n. For the negative route, one explicit delta>0 and an unbounded horizon sequence with certified W<= (1-delta)U^ZJ is required. Neither theorem-strength obligation is reduced to an easier established interface.

# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Signed-closed components:
- Expected-orthogonal-gradient legal adversaries have expected normalized error at most beta_n<=2/n^2, uniformly in the schedule; n^2 beta_n->2 — fact 0e7e78056dc6258c.
- Under the specified ConPD joins and primitive-prefix sum caps, a fixed finite library of terminal seeds satisfies W_N/U_N^ZJ >= (1+C_E/sqrt(2S_bullet(N)+1))^(-2) — fact 0be8b54e0a33381c.

| Route | Status | Reason and exact scope |
|---|---|---|
| Orthogonal moments as a target-scale adversary | FALSE AS STATED | Fact 0e7e78056dc6258c gives an n^-2 ceiling only for that distribution class |
| Fixed terminal-library replacement under the old joins | FALSE AS STATED | Fact 0be8b54e0a33381c forces any fixed relative gain to disappear |
| Universal complex lens instead of the actual schedule-dependent domain | DANGEROUS | Shared exact candidate 9378a73359d8ad5b constructs a Chebyshev obstruction; not accepted |
| Fixed chronological window as an exact conditional state | DANGEROUS | Shared candidate 2a21f2929be856fc omits a decisive old value; not accepted |
| Symmetric positive-deficit scalar primitive recursion | DANGEROUS | Shared candidate 79da55e26c4a7406 predicts order 1/n; not accepted |

The unverified rows do not exclude their broader mechanism families.

# 2. Interface contract table

## Correlated geometric realization
Input required: a proposed Gram/covariance law and points satisfying GD, every global projection or smooth-convex interpolation inequality, a finite-dimensional realization, and normalized initial distance.
Output claimed: a legal full-function adversary with an explicit objective lower bound.
Available facts: 0e7e78056dc6258c excludes the orthogonal special case; it does not supply a correlated construction.
Missing verification: a construction for arbitrary varying h and its quantitative value.
Failure mode: checking only adjacent projections or expected orthogonality can miss the required history constraints.
Status: PARTIAL.

## Polynomial / chronological comparison
Input required: full schedule-dependent cycle constraints or two-sided history envelopes, before maximization.
Output claimed: a quantitative lower bound comparable to U_n^ZJ.
Available facts: none for this interface.
Missing verification: a new estimate beyond equivalent full-PEP encoding; all schedules and all large horizons.
Failure mode: a universal spectral lens may be too small, while an unconstrained polynomial Gram parameterization may merely rename the original problem.
Status: PARTIAL.

## Improved family propagation
Input required: a legal saturated primitive improvement, richer invariant or different join rule, and exact upper-bound certificates.
Output claimed: one fixed delta>0 of asymptotic improvement below the actual benchmark.
Available facts: 0be8b54e0a33381c eliminates the fixed terminal-only path.
Missing verification: a concrete surviving gain, its infinite-family propagation, and benchmark comparison.
Failure mode: a finite seed gain can vanish; a relaxed certificate can lose the desired exponent.
Status: PARTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "Random gradients are approximately independent." Conditional only: the accepted orthogonal-moment theorem shows that this exact special case is too weak; controlled temporal correlations matter.
- "Full-history envelopes characterize the process." Conditional only: characterization is not a quantitative minimax comparison.
- "A polynomial basis spans every transcript Gram matrix." Method shortcut, not a proof step: an equivalent parameterization does not prove the missing uniform inequality.
- "A better one-step sum gives a better asymptotic schedule." Not automatic: the accepted terminal obstruction and unverified scalar-deficit calculation identify distinct propagation failures.
- Do not strengthen an accepted restricted obstruction to all finite-dimensional state or probability methods.
- Do not treat small values of a lower-bound channel as an upper bound on W.
- Do not repeat verifier submissions for elementary support or minor variants.
- Do not replace U_n^ZJ by the decimal 0.4239.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

## Bridge 1 — Correlated construction (independent)
Target statement: a specified schedule-dependent correlated process yields legal finite-dimensional GD data and an explicit normalized objective lower bound.
Prerequisites: actual covariance/face data, not a formal distribution.
Existing facts: 0e7e78056dc6258c excludes zero temporal correlations as the desired mechanism.
Missing checks: global inequalities, dimension, objective and parameter dependence.
Closure criterion: a complete construction and a nontrivial quantitative bound; any uniform profile comparison remains separate until proved.

## Bridge 2 — Quantitative cut or cycle invariant (independent)
Target statement: an explicit invariant on the already specified full cut/cycle state gives a proved new inequality controlling a legal objective witness.
Prerequisites: actual invariant formula and sound retained state.
Existing facts: none.
Missing checks: arbitrary schedule transitions, normalization, and quantitative relation to the exact benchmark.
Closure criterion: an inequality not obtained by assuming (Q) or an equivalent minimax assertion.

## Bridge 3 — Surviving upper-bound improvement (independent alternative)
Target statement: a concrete new schedule family has a certified objective upper bound with a fixed relative advantage over U_n^ZJ on unbounded horizons.
Prerequisites: an actual improved primitive, richer potential or changed composition rule.
Existing facts: 0be8b54e0a33381c specifies one class that cannot supply this.
Missing checks: exact feasibility and nonvanishing propagation.
Closure criterion: a full family proof; a finite improvement alone does not close the bridge.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.** Neither the uniform exact-profile lower bound nor a certified asymptotic improvement has been established.

The fixed goal is unchanged: with `W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_*)/(L||x_0-x_*||^2)`, `W_n^*=inf_{h in [0,infinity)^n} W_n(h)`, and the exact supplied Zhang--Jiang dynamic-programming sequence `B_n=2S_bullet(n)+1`, prove `W_n^* B_n -> 1`, or prove a fixed relative improvement on unbounded horizons with legal full-class upper certificates. A positive resolution requires a threshold independent of the entire nonnegative schedule. The exponent `rho=log_2(1+sqrt(2))` alone is not the goal.

The signed-closed components are five scoped tools or obstructions, listed below. None closes a target-level sub-task. A viable conditional negative architecture is now explicit: a primitive seed of length r with `A=1+sum(h)>(r+1)^rho` would amplify into an asymptotic separation. The seed is unknown, and the amplification argument itself remains a shared, unverified derivation. On the positive side, full interpolation certificates and nonseparable adversaries are available as frameworks, but the quantitative comparison remains unmatched.

The main blocker is not a source-normalization issue or a missing routine interpolation citation. It is the absence of either a legal witness uniformly strong enough for all schedules, or an actual improved infinite schedule family. The highest-priority unresolved interfaces are full-matrix descent without triangular restrictions and residual-preserving composition without a fixed constant loss. Existing exclusions are **method failure**, not **proposition failure**. Do not interpret PARTIAL or SUBSTANTIAL as almost solved.

Current best conditional proof skeleton (negative alternative):
1. Produce a nonnegative primitive seed h of length r with shifted mass A greater than `(r+1)^rho`; no such seed is known.
2. Supply its exact primitive-potential certificate for every smooth convex trajectory, including all interpolation and sign conditions.
3. Use correctly matched ConPP/ConPD closure theorems to replace every primitive leaf of an optimal dominant DP tree by that seed.
4. Keep the unique terminal dominant leaf empty; the resulting length is `(r+1)n`.
5. Use the square-root nonexpansiveness in fact `0be8b54e0a33381c` to control the unmatched terminal leaf by the fixed additive error `sqrt(A)-1`.
6. Prove the finite positive limsup of `B_n/n^rho`, and compare the same input subsequence with its multiplied output horizons. The shared amplification derivation supplies this argument but is not yet a fact.
7. Obtain a strict ratio at most `(r+1)^rho/A<1`, then independently attack the complete object and verify the full certificate chain.

Central missing lemma for this skeleton: there exist an integer r>=1 and h in `[0,infinity)^r`, with s=sum(h), such that `1+s>(r+1)^rho` and, for every finite-dimensional convex 1-smooth function with a minimizer and every initial point, its GD trajectory satisfies

`V_h = 2 sum_{i<r} h_i(f_i-f_r) + sum_{i<r} h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2 - s(s+1)||g_r||^2 >= 0`.

This is an unresolved existence statement, not an established property of a discovered seed. It may be false. A seed merely beating its finite recursive DP value without crossing the displayed power threshold is not covered by this skeleton. Failure of this sufficient route would not prove the positive answer.

# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Signed-closed components:
- Under the specified unchanged ConPD joins and primitive-prefix mass caps, a fixed finite terminal library cannot preserve a fixed relative asymptotic improvement. The same fact includes the universal Huber lower bound and scalar square-root transport identity -- `0be8b54e0a33381c`.
- Legal random adversaries with pairwise orthogonal gradient moments have expected normalized error at most `beta_n<=2/n^2` -- `0e7e78056dc6258c`.
- Exact single-cut asymmetric Huber witnesses give `K_n<=W_n`, and an explicit family makes K_n too weak -- `b98bb807d514e80d`.
- Complex prefix-polynomial transcripts have an exact all-cycle realization criterion; the smaller universal lens plus Huber is too weak -- `dfde2fa326c908bf`.
- The retained-offset alternating-ray construction gives `K_n<=F_ray_n<=W_n` on its fully stated feasible paths -- `1fca3e5dd555cb4a`.

| Universal complex lens as a sharp witness | FALSE AS STATED | Fact `dfde2fa326c908bf`; the schedule-dependent legal domain remains open |
| Finite terminal seed library with unchanged joins | FALSE AS STATED | Fact `0be8b54e0a33381c`; richer joins and new primitive capacities remain open |
| Outward rays plus quadratics plus one terminal cap | DANGEROUS | Shared recursive counterfamily derivation, not an accepted fact and not an upper bound on true W |
| Homogeneous two-curvature or quadratic-prefix/no-crossing suffix repair | DANGEROUS | Shared family-specific obstructions; arbitrary scalar recrossings are not excluded |
| Aggregate-only, fixed-residual, balanced whole-tail transport | FALSE AS STATED | Shared exact certificate calculation yields a fixed benchmark loss; this concerns that certificate rule, not true schedule performance |
| Re-deriving attainment and KKT as the quantitative breakthrough | OBSOLETE | Historical overlap was explicitly acknowledged; no profile comparison follows from these identities alone |

# 2. Interface contract table

### Full interpolation to joint descent
**Input required.** A complete tight certificate `(q,lambda,h)` with nonnegative interpolation weights, exact objective flow, and the full quadratic form `M=q||x_0||^2-2f_n+sum lambda_ij Q_ij` positive semidefinite after substituting the actual GD recursion. Here `Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2<=0`.

**Output claimed.** The shared suffix-deformation calculation gives strict improvement when finite-index flow is triangular and its later cut profiles have a stated proportionality. The proportionality is automatic with two remaining steps. Earlier results cover certain final-gradient Schur directions and transferable star-outgoing flow.

**Available facts.** No accepted fact asserts this new deformation or those structural hypotheses. Fact `dfde2fa326c908bf` is a different realization tool and does not fill this gap.

**Missing verification on the actual model.** (1) Remove or prove the triangular restriction. (2) Handle incompatible later cut profiles. (3) Connect any resulting global Huber-tightness statement to the exact DP mass cap; tightness alone is not the target.

**Failure mode if ignored.** Forward edges create mixed matrix entries under step changes. Dropping them or checking only a small principal block gives a false full certificate. Local stationary tuples cannot be promoted to global minimizers.

**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Correlated geometric data to a uniform legal witness
**Input required.** One finite-dimensional smooth convex function, all cross-time interpolation inequalities and an actual minimizer. In the Moreau realization, all old and future supporting planes must be dominated at each proposed proximal point, with retained offsets and function values.

**Output claimed.** Shared constructions realize nonseparable Markov and arc relays for explicit schedule families. Positive checkpoint values repair one genuine signed-history failure; closest-minimizer normalization improves some witnesses.

**Available facts.** `0e7e78056dc6258c` excludes orthogonal moments only. `b98bb807d514e80d` and `1fca3e5dd555cb4a` give legal scalar baseline witnesses.

**Missing verification on the actual model.** (1) Quantitatively choose the data for general nonperiodic schedules. (2) Retain every history constraint through recursive scales. (3) Obtain a uniform relative loss tending to zero against B_n.

**Failure mode if ignored.** An explicit large-error periodic example can remain far from the difficult near-critical schedules. Positive offsets, PSD Gram matrices, or local correlation inequalities alone do not establish all-plane legality.

**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Scalar interval reachability to a strong fixed function
**Input required.** A single nondecreasing 1-Lipschitz g on `[0,infinity)` with g(0)=0, its odd extension, and the fixed even function `f(x)=integral_0^{|x|} g(r)dr`.

**Output claimed.** The shared interval-image derivation tracks `R_(t+1)=max_{0<=r<=R_t}|r-h_t g(r)|` exactly and yields a single-initial-point witness with payoff `2 integral_0^{R_n}g/R_0^2`. Optimizing R_0 recovers the exact value for that fixed function.

**Available facts.** No accepted fact yet covers this new interval package. `1fca3e5dd555cb4a` covers a different scalar construction.

**Missing verification on the actual model.** Choose one globally compatible multi-knot derivative that produces the required area. No full scalar-class profile lower bound has been derived.

**Failure mode if ignored.** Optimizing the derivative independently at each time silently changes the function. Exact interval dynamics do not justify a minimax exchange between the fixed function and timewise controls.

**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Rich endpoint composition to a competitive asymptotic certificate
**Input required.** Nonnegative actual interpolation multipliers, the complete PSD residual, and enough boundary variables to retain both objective and primitive dual residuals through a bridge.

**Output claimed.** Shared whole-tail identities supply exact finite certificates. The tested aggregate-only update forces a terminal residual reset and loses a fixed constant under the analyzed balanced recursion.

**Available facts.** `0be8b54e0a33381c` supplies the scoped scalar comparison; it does not justify a richer endpoint composition law.

**Missing verification on the actual model.** (1) Retain the endpoint terms lost by aggregation. (2) Recover the known recursive benchmark as a calibration. (3) Prove a fixed improvement on unbounded horizons, with exact certificates.

**Failure mode if ignored.** A positive residual and a finite valid example can coexist with a uniformly inferior asymptotic certificate. Signed interval coordinates are not permission to use negative interpolation weights.

**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Improved primitive seed to the actual negative resolution
**Input required.** The precise primitive potential and above-power inequality stated in section 0; correctly matched ConPP/ConPD closure theorems.

**Output claimed.** The shared amplification derivation gives length `(r+1)n` and denominator at least `[sqrt(A B_n)-(sqrt(A)-1)]^2`, then a strict subsequential profile improvement.

**Available facts.** `0be8b54e0a33381c` supplies the nonexpansive scalar transport used to handle the unique terminal leaf.

**Missing verification on the actual model.** The qualifying seed is absent. The final amplification and external closure inputs are not a completed accepted target package.

**Failure mode if ignored.** A finite gain smaller than the above-power threshold has no asymptotic consequence from this argument; a failed finite search is not a nonexistence theorem.

**Status.** SUBSTANTIAL for the conditional interface only; the negative resolution remains PARTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- **DANGEROUS:** "The remaining generalization is just removal of triangularity." It changes the mixed entries of the full PSD form and is an unmatched structural hypothesis, not notation.
- **DANGEROUS:** "The interval state removes all history." It removes trajectory reachability history only after one entire legal scalar function is fixed; monotonicity, slopes and accumulated area remain global constraints.
- **DANGEROUS:** "A primitive seed better than the recursive value disproves the conjecture." The actual amplification condition is stronger, or requires a different exact phase comparison.
- **DANGEROUS:** "The new periodic relay proves sharpness of the Silver constant." It proves a restricted-family witness. Its parameters have not been selected uniformly for arbitrary schedules.
- **DANGEROUS:** "No improved seed appeared, so the benchmark is optimal." A finite numerical search cannot establish an all-length, all-certificate assertion.
- Do not reuse orthogonal moments or the universal complex lens without a mechanism escaping their accepted obstructions.
- Do not replace full interpolation by adjacent cocoercivity or variable curvature matrices chosen independently in time.
- Do not discard signed-history costs or the terminal function-value area in a recursive construction.
- Do not identify recursive composability with the ambient class of all primitive or all schedules.
- Do not replace the exact B_n sequence by a decimal constant or assume convergence of its normalized phase.
- Do not interpret a fixed loss in one upper-certificate rule as a lower bound on the schedules it analyzes.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge 1 -- an actual above-power primitive seed
**Target statement.** The r,h,s and universal potential inequality in section 0, with `1+s>(r+1)^rho` and nonnegative h.

**Prerequisites.** The actual primitive definition, full smooth-convex interpolation, exact multiplier signs and PSD verification, and the same objective normalization.

**Existing facts to use.** `0be8b54e0a33381c` for the terminal-leaf transport in the subsequent amplification, not for existence of the seed.

**Missing checks.** A concrete seed; an exact certificate; the complete external closure chain. Finite numerical non-discovery supplies none of these.

**Closure criterion.** A specific finite schedule with an algebraic/rational certificate proving the universal potential and the strict power inequality. This bridge is independent of the affirmative route; the amplification step depends on it. A scoped nonexistence theorem would close only its stated seed class, not the original goal.

### Bridge 2 -- full feasible descent above the Huber value
**Target statement.** For any positive globally minimizing schedule h with optimal tight value `q>tau(h)=1/(1+2sum h)` and a complete matching primal-dual certificate, construct a same-length nonnegative h' and a full-class certificate with `q'<q`, or identify an exact obstruction that invalidates this proposed descent theorem. No triangular-flow or cut-proportionality hypothesis may be assumed without proof on the actual minimizing tuple.

**Prerequisites.** Attainment and complete primal-dual matching, with all interpolation, full PSD, complementarity and inactive-edge constraints retained. The existing stationarity derivation is shared candidate material, not a substitute for these checks.

**Existing facts to use.** `0be8b54e0a33381c` supplies the universal Huber baseline tau. No accepted fact supplies the unrestricted descent.

**Missing checks.** Forward-edge terms, cut-profile incompatibility, kernel behavior, and the possible difference between stationary and globally minimizing schedules. Even successful descent still leaves the exact DP mass bound for the remaining tight class.

**Closure criterion.** An actual feasible strict descent formula under the full hypotheses, not a definition of a desired direction; alternatively, an exact matching tuple disproving a proposed local implication with its scope carefully stated. This bridge is independent of the negative seed route. It is a substantial unresolved mathematical statement, not a routine perturbation exercise.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.** The exact uniform lower comparison and a certified asymptotic improvement are both absent.

The fixed target is unchanged. For nonnegative predetermined GD steps h of length n, let W_n(h) be the supremum of 2(f(x_n)-f_*)/(L||x_0-x_*||^2) over all finite-dimensional smooth convex instances with a minimizer, and let W_n^*=inf_h W_n(h). Define the exact benchmark in shifted coordinates by P_0=B_0=1 and

P_n=max_(k+l=n-1) F(P_k,P_l),
B_n=max_(k+l=n-1) G(P_k,B_l),
F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2.

Thus P_n=1+S_circle(n), B_n=1+2S_bullet(n), and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n. The positive target is: for every epsilon>0 there is an N_epsilon, independent of h, such that W_n(h)B_n>=1-epsilon for every n>=N_epsilon and every h>=0. A negative resolution requires an actual fixed relative improvement on unbounded horizons, with valid full-class upper certificates.

Six scoped components are now signed-closed. The new unrestricted component, fact 1252b8823bb5a290, gives exact unqueried-terminal reduction and selected full certificates for every nonnegative schedule, including n=0. It closes that interface; it gives no profile lower bound. The most explicit existing conditional negative chain still requires an unknown above-power primitive seed. A geometric profile state and richer certificate states are concrete candidates, but no choice of their parameters achieves the target comparison.

The main blocker is quantitative coverage of arbitrary schedules, not an omitted routine interpolation theorem. The highest-priority unresolved interface is conversion of the exact terminal/prefix representation or a legal geometric state into a constant-preserving comparison with B_n. The recorded exclusions are **method failure**, not **proposition failure**. PARTIAL and SUBSTANTIAL do not mean almost solved.

Current best explicit conditional proof skeleton (a negative alternative, not a prediction):
1. Find a primitive seed h of length r with A=1+sum(h)>(r+1)^rho, rho=log_2(1+sqrt(2)). No such seed is known.
2. Prove its universal primitive potential inequality exactly, with all signs and interpolation conditions.
3. Match the complete ConPP/ConPD closure inputs and replace every primitive leaf of a maximizing dominant DP tree by the seed.
4. Keep the unique terminal dominant leaf empty; the resulting length is (r+1)n.
5. Use fact 0be8b54e0a33381c to control the unmatched terminal leaf in square-root mass by sqrt(A)-1.
6. Complete the conditional amplification argument, including its finite positive benchmark limsup and the same input/output subsequence comparison.
7. Obtain a ratio at most (r+1)^rho/A<1 and verify the complete assembled argument after an independent attack.

Central missing lemma for this particular chain: there exist r>=1 and h in [0,infinity)^r, with s=sum h and 1+s>(r+1)^rho, such that every convex 1-smooth GD trajectory satisfies

V_h=2sum_(i<r)h_i(f_i-f_r)+sum_(i<r)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g_r||^2>=0.

This existence statement may be false. The rejected eleven-step seed does not satisfy it. A seed merely better than its finite recursive DP value but below the displayed power threshold is not covered by this chain. Failure of this sufficient route would not establish the positive resolution.

# 2. Interface contract table

**Input required.** A finite nonnegative schedule, the optimum sample, every ordered smooth-convex interpolation inequality in the queried prefix, and outgoing terminal inequalities. **Output claimed.** The explicit maximal prefix interpolant realizes the exact reduced value. **Input required.** The actual boundary gradient magnitude G, center and unit outgoing direction; the old-plane envelope Phi; the old-query acceptance envelope Psi; the constrained distance function Dist; a nonempty true zero set; and fresh coordinates orthogonal to the entire old ambient data. **Output claimed.** The draft max_three_profile_state.md gives exact updates and a complete three-cell example. **Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 may complete a genuinely unqueried final sample; no accepted fact yet covers the new profile-valued update. **Missing verification on the actual model.** A constructive parameter/checkpoint policy for arbitrary prescribed schedules with a uniform near-sharp payoff. **Failure mode if ignored.** Omitting G already produces identical stored profiles with different next GD points. Forgetting old planes, old queries, zero-set feasibility or the actual distance loses legality or normalization. The derived level/scale budgets are real losses.

**Input required.** Actual nonnegative interpolation multipliers, complete function-value and proximal-point moments, and a valid final objective certificate. **Output claimed.** Drafts recover F and G exactly, construct finite residual states and identify signed slack quantities that survive tight equality modes. **Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 gives a full terminal closure; 0be8b54e0a33381c supplies the scoped asymptotic comparison tool. **Missing verification on the actual model.** A sign-controlled multi-bridge closure and a strict asymptotic improvement. **Failure mode if ignored.** The tested finite-residual scalar transport is strictly G-dominated. Enlarging a PSD gradient-memory vector cannot bypass the stated intrinsic-class rigidity. Endpoint data also fail to determine every all-cross interpolation sum.

### Scalar multiscale construction to target-scale error -- PARTIAL
**Input required.** One globally fixed monotone 1-Lipschitz derivative, complete areas and a valid minimizer, with the permitted asymmetry and horizon-dependent complexity stated. **Output claimed.** Exact interval dynamics are available as a draft representation. Several nested, staircase and discretely homogeneous classes have draft obstructions on H_m. **Available facts.** b98bb807d514e80d and 1fca3e5dd555cb4a cover narrower legal witnesses. **Missing verification on the actual model.** A successful nonperiodic/general asymmetric construction, or a correctly scoped stronger obstruction. **Failure mode if ignored.** Arbitrarily many knots inside an excluded structured class do not cover all scalar functions; no scalar obstruction is an upper bound on unrestricted multidimensional W.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- **DANGEROUS:** "The last-sample deletion can be repeated backwards." Queried gradients control later positions; the accepted proof does not remove their incoming inequalities.
- **DANGEROUS:** "An optimal dual can always be made triangular." The explicit global two-step draft counterexample retains an adjacent forward edge even in its unique optimal certificate.

It does not automatically close the n-step minimax problem. This bridge is independent of the finite-seed search.

| Every normal spectral mixture repairs the ruler obstruction | FALSE AS STATED | Draft22347ffeb25f8830 includes joint repair of individually illegal modes; genuinely nonnormal operators remain outside it |
| Arbitrarily ill-conditioned single-real-eigenvalue Jordan order2 repairs that family | FALSE AS STATED | Draftd4ae45931cfc72a3 uses actual value coercivity and derivative damping; mixtures of different eigenvalues are outside its scope |
| The explicit radical eight-step seed is primitive | FALSE AS STATED | Independent exact drafts759b0f50d34487a6 and dd3ca4162bb552a3 give negative legal intrinsic energy |
| Every signed-credit full certificate directly restricts to a nonnegative tail certificate | FALSE AS STATED | Draftf7cee3034df8cb7f has an explicit full PSD counterexample to the coefficient floor; re-selection is a different question |
| Earlier Chebyshev terminal examples separate every accepted baseline | FALSE AS STATED | Correctionec8567340a9f5e45 shows the accepted alternating-ray family already handles those examples |
| Repeating exact projection reduction or last-stage minimax reformulations | OBSOLETE | Concrete draft representations now exist; the missing information is quantitative |

Every draft row above remains outside the accepted fact graph. Each exclusion retains the hypotheses of its actual construction class.

# 2. Interface contract table

### Exact terminal completion
**Input required.** The optimum and every ordered interpolation inequality on the genuinely queried prefix, together with outgoing terminal inequalities.
**Output claimed.** The maximal prefix interpolant realizes the exact attained terminal value; some full optimal certificate has a terminal square plus the complete prefix PSD remainder.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290, including zero and empty schedules.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** None for this exact component.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Repeating the deletion at queried samples changes subsequent GD positions and need not be valid.
**Status.** CLOSED.

### Primitive sign to projection geometry
**Input required.** A legal negative intrinsic-energy trajectory with its original nonnegative steps and all function values.
**Output claimed.** Drafts4ad60ca9f3ec3e2e and846c8ad5e763dfb7 give finite projection/simplex witnesses, retaining all normal inequalities and the negative sign. For F_C(x)=(||x||^2-dist(x,C)^2)/2, the intrinsic energy becomes ||x_0||^2-||x_n+S P_C(x_n)||^2.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 supplies interpolation background; the projection reductions themselves are drafts.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Complete reduction acceptance, and a quantitative relation between defect and the true closest-minimizer distance along growing horizons.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A translated-triangle example keeps negative raw energy while its normalized objective tends to zero.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Correlated projection walk to a capacity or objective inequality
**Input required.** One real Gram realization satisfying every projection normal inequality against every vertex and zero, with the actual chronological GD recursion.
**Output claimed.** Only scoped constructions and finite calibrations are currently available. C29 rules out a reduction to every quadratic and single cut.
**Available facts.** 0e7e78056dc6258c identifies the orthogonal limitation; 1fca3e5dd555cb4a and b98bb807d514e80d provide baseline comparators.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** A correlated family with target-relevant payoff and eventual arbitrary-schedule coverage.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A positive Gram matrix, code identity, probability distribution or local cocoercivity alone does not imply full interpolation.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Chronological nonnormal prefixes to a final-step lower witness
**Input required.** The same actual positions, gradients, legal values, real PSD geometry and every prefix cycle, with a terminal test vector satisfying all anchors.
**Output claimed.** Draft normal-mixture and real-Jordan obstructions narrow the remaining structured ansatz. Elliptic metrics and more general nonnormal correlation are not excluded by those drafts.
**Available facts.** dfde2fa326c908bf and1252b8823bb5a290.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** An explicit unbounded-horizon legal family beating the old channels at the required scale, or a complete obstruction for the next specified ansatz.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Individual-mode legality is not necessary for joint legality; conversely an arbitrary kernel is only an SDP reparameterization.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Signed terminal credit to a useful upper certificate
**Input required.** For a tail b, E_theta=V_b-theta sum_i b_i Q_(i,m), 0<=theta<1, with a complete nonnegative interpolation certificate and full PSD remainder. Saturated joins retain their actual lengths and all old query edges.
**Output claimed.** Draftedbbe4af2a2ad5c2 gives two-bridge composition and a final objective certificate. Its explicit calibration is strictly dominated and does not show cone enlargement.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 for final closure; 0be8b54e0a33381c for the limited unchanged-library obstruction.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** A tail for which the credit genuinely helps, sufficient physical capacity, and a valid infinite-horizon comparison; alternatively a general re-selection theorem.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Signed coordinates cannot replace nonnegative actual multipliers. The <=2-step, local-silver and fixed-middle2 exclusions do not prove general re-selection.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- **DANGEROUS:** "Primitive failure gives a uniform objective improvement." Its negative energy can survive a minimizer change while the initial-distance denominator grows. Draft20c6d21ee60a4cb0 provides a defect conversion with explicit instance-dependent losses, not the uniform profile theorem.
- **DANGEROUS:** "Every quadratic and every cut passing implies primitive validity." Draftc58273e20409955e shows those necessary tests admit arbitrarily excessive capacity. Nonlinear correlated geometry remains required.
- **DANGEROUS:** "The last-sample deletion can be repeated backwards." Fact1252b8823bb5a290 retains all queried-prefix constraints.
- **DANGEROUS:** "The four local silver witnesses globalize." Draft66067b85d1228261 passes all four at silver capacity but is defeated by an endpoint cut.
- **DANGEROUS:** "Failure of normal mixtures means all polynomial methods fail." Nonnormal correlations, higher-order clocks and more general structured kernels are outside that exclusion.
- **DANGEROUS:** "A fixed-prefix last-step minimax exchange optimizes every step." The fixed-prefix mixture result and the joint nonconvexity counterexample do not authorize earlier exchanges.

Do not discard old planes, old query heights or the true minimizer cost. Do not optimize an already dominated scalar transport without changing its mechanism. Do not replace the exact B_n profile by a decimal. Do not infer unrestricted upper bounds from failed lower-witness families. Do not promote a complete-looking draft to a fact. Do not equate finite-horizon capacity calibration with asymptotic minimax resolution.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge 1 -- strict signed-credit enlargement or general repair
**Target statement.** Either exhibit finite b>=0 and theta in[0,1) with a complete nonnegative full-PSD certificate for E_theta and a legal negative V_b witness, or prove an explicitly quantified re-selection implication from such E_theta certificates to ordinary primitive certificates for every b in a stated class.
**Prerequisites.** The precise Q and V conventions above and all actual multiplier signs. Validity and certifiability must not be identified without a proof for this cone.
**Existing facts to use.** 1252b8823bb5a290 for final objective conversion when applicable.
**Missing checks.** Actual cone separation or PSD-preserving repair; useful physical capacity if separation succeeds.
**Closure criterion.** A concrete separated object or complete repair theorem. The step from separation to an asymptotic improvement is additional. Independent of the lower-witness bridges.

### Bridge 2 -- correlated walk on the two-copy ruler
**Target statement.** For c=13/5, a=c^(-17), H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,a c^j,H_j), construct finite polytopes C_m containing zero and actual GD starts for G_m=(H_m,H_m) with negative intrinsic energy, with explicit defect and initial-distance bounds as m grows; or prove a precise obstruction for a specified correlated construction class.
**Prerequisites.** Every normal inequality, real PSD realization, and fixed-function GD chronology.
**Existing facts to use.** 0e7e78056dc6258c for the orthogonal boundary and1252b8823bb5a290 for legal terminal completion.
**Missing checks.** The correlated construction and its quantitative normalization; C29 supplies no general primitive certificate.
**Closure criterion.** A genuine growing-horizon legal object or whole-class obstruction. Even a negative primitive witness alone does not settle the original objective target. Independent of Bridge1.

### Bridge 3 -- objective payoff in the near-critical regime
**Target statement.** Let lambda=1+sqrt2, c_m=lambda(1+1/m), a_m=(1-1/m)/c_m, H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_m c_m^j,H_j) for j<m, and p_m be H_m without its last entry. Determine a rigorous lower bound for inf_(b>=0)W_(2^m-1)(p_m,b) relative to the exact B_(2^m-1), accounting for the full quadratic spectrum and all accepted baselines first.
**Prerequisites.** Parameters are fixed over each whole horizon; every adversary is globally legal and its true initial-distance denominator is used.
**Existing facts to use.** 1252b8823bb5a290, b98bb807d514e80d and1fca3e5dd555cb4a.
**Missing checks.** A constant-scale payoff or a precise demonstrated obstruction. No target ratio is assumed.
**Closure criterion.** A quantitative result for this actual family. Arbitrary-schedule/all-horizon coverage remains separate. Independent of Bridges1 and2.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.** Exact terminal completion is available, but neither a uniform sharp lower witness nor an asymptotically improving full-class upper certificate has been obtained.

The fixed goal is unchanged: for predetermined nonnegative GD steps, with
W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_*)/(L||x_0-x_*||^2), decide whether W_n^*/U_n^ZJ tends to one. The affirmative statement requires every epsilon>0 to admit N_epsilon independent of h such that W_n(h)>=(1-epsilon)U_n^ZJ for every n>=N_epsilon and every h in [0,infinity)^n. A negative resolution requires a fixed delta>0 and certified schedules on unbounded horizons with W_n(h)<=(1-delta)U_n^ZJ.

For the exact comparator, P_0=B_0=1,
F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2,
P_n=max_(k+l=n-1)F(P_k,P_l), B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(P_k,B_l), and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n. No decimal constant or assumed normalized limit replaces this sequence.

Closed components are the six scoped facts listed below. The viable architectures are a full legal correlated adversary with quantitative normalization, or a genuinely stronger upper-certificate composition. The main blocker is a target-scale consequence of the exact representations: a sign-preserving primitive reduction has no automatic objective-scale guarantee. The highest-priority unresolved bridge in the constructive upper route is an actual useful enlargement by signed terminal credit, or a theorem excluding that enlargement. Current evidence is **method failure**, not proposition failure: several entire construction classes fail on specified schedules, but unrestricted W remains undetermined.

Calibration caveat: PARTIAL and SUBSTANTIAL do not mean almost solved; the quantitative interfaces remain unmatched.

The newest scoped evidence makes the constant-scale obstruction sharper. On the exact near-critical word defined in Bridge2 below, draftbf591e52671f6d33 bounds every retained zero-height two-block prefix followed by any legal constant-gradient tail, over its entire angle range, by (5949/6088)K, where K=1/(2 sum H_m+1). Together with the listed ray, lens and full quadratic baselines, the resulting benchmark ratio has limit superior at most2/e. This is an obstruction to those mechanisms, never an upper bound on unrestricted W. Draft8010ea721f41d0d3 shows that positive checkpoint height without its location can still lead to zero terminal error. Actual varying-tail terminal correlation, or an attained positive-height/position continuation, is therefore a concrete missing interface.

Draft295385c862b91a07 excludes every common horizon-dependent SPD metric in the compact parabolic regime z_m=1-u_m/m+i v_m/sqrt(m), with bounded u_m and v_m in a fixed positive compact interval, even allowing legal values and free completion. Other frequency regimes and more general correlations remain open. Draft4aaee6131a92da0e excludes signed-certificate repair that holds the supplied full PSD remainder fixed; a different ordinary certificate exists for its example. Draft347ec0d726695de2 excludes one simultaneous energy-preserving core shear because it creates a positive old interpolation residual. Neither proves strict cone enlargement nor rules out variable-matrix re-selection.

1. Produce a finite nonnegative seed b of length r with primitive capacity A=1+sum b>(r+1)^rho, rho=log_2(1+sqrt2).
2. Supply a nonnegative complete interpolation/PSD certificate for its intrinsic primitive energy; passing necessary cuts or all quadratics is insufficient.
3. Apply exact balanced primitive joins, preserving every physical step and every multiplier.
4. Convert to last-iterate objective certificates with the actual initial-distance term, using the terminal closure of fact1252b8823bb5a290 where applicable.
5. Prove the unbounded-horizon comparison to the exact B_n profile, including all constants in the amplification argument.
6. Obtain a fixed relative improvement and hence disprove the goal. No seed satisfying step1 is known. The amplification remains a draft conditional package, not a verified conclusion.

Central missing lemma for this particular architecture: there exist a finite r, b in [0,infinity)^r, and nonnegative coefficients on the complete ordered smooth-convex interpolation inequalities such that the intrinsic primitive energy has a full PSD identity and 1+sum b>(r+1)^rho. This existential claim may be false. It is not presented as a necessary condition for every possible negative answer, and its absence does not support the affirmative answer.

The complete C_spine repair candidate2d75b4696641736c applies to finite signed certificates on schedules with recursively saturated right-tail cuts ending in at most two positive steps; it forces ordinary earlier-prefix certificates and may change the full PSD remainder. Arbitrary schedules need not have those cuts, and universal validity is not identified with certifiability by this result.

The near-critical free-terminal relaxation is now addressed by draft70e9da73936cb7b3: two queried-constant cells give K/(1-d0^2 K), preserving the same asymptotic gap from1/B_n. The retained-prefix three-cell, path/tree, tested four-phase DAG, and stated fresh-antialigned Markov families also remain too weak in their exact scopes. The Markov exclusion now allows every later affine intercept, positive interior height and fresh innovation norm. It does not cover mixed signs, genuinely longer memory, general within-gap gradient variation or arbitrary initial geometry. Ordinary Huber refutes an unchanged strictly-below-K extension to all positive adjacent coefficients.

The fixed13/5 scalar-clock and whole-normal-mixture results concern a DIFFERENT schedule from the new1+a_m c_m^j word. They cannot be imported by changing one symbol. Candidate35d2f3dd8f474b19 gives an explicit polynomial counterexample to the old damping estimate on the new word; it is not claimed to be a legal prefix. Jointly legal mixtures of individually illegal modes therefore require their own argument. Draftcd74f7b37ded4d52 identifies another exact claimed obstruction: nonnegative initial-direction projections together with pairwise nonnegative transverse correlations yield the Huber bound, with a quantified free-terminal extension. Realizability still requires the same full prefix, not separate checks on individual modes.

The general reversal proposal remains conditional. Full slack congruence in H-duality does not by itself preserve actual multiplier signs. The available noncrossing-arc classification concerns optimal vertex fixed-point methods, not arbitrary intrinsic GD certificates. Its graphs encode dual multipliers, unlike the primal coordinate-flow graphs in the relay exclusions. No lossless noncrossing or recursive decomposition of arbitrary GD schedules has been proved.

The newest scoped evidence makes the constant-scale obstruction sharper. On the exact near-critical word defined in Bridge2 below, draftbf591e52671f6d33 bounds every retained zero-height two-block prefix followed by any legal constant-gradient tail, over its entire angle range, by (5949/6088)K, where K=1/(2 sum H_m+1). Together with the listed ray, lens and full quadratic baselines, the resulting benchmark ratio has limit superior at most2/e. This is an obstruction to those mechanisms, never an upper bound on unrestricted W. Draft8010ea721f41d0d3 shows that positive checkpoint height without its location can still lead to zero terminal error. Actual varying-tail terminal correlation, or an attained positive-height/position continuation, is therefore a concrete missing interface.

Draft295385c862b91a07 excludes every common horizon-dependent SPD metric in the compact parabolic regime z_m=1-u_m/m+i v_m/sqrt(m), with bounded u_m and v_m in a fixed positive compact interval, even allowing legal values and free completion. Other frequency regimes and more general correlations remain open. Draft4aaee6131a92da0e excludes signed-certificate repair that holds the supplied full PSD remainder fixed; a different ordinary certificate exists for its example. Draft347ec0d726695de2 excludes one simultaneous energy-preserving core shear because it creates a positive old interpolation residual. Neither proves strict cone enlargement nor rules out variable-matrix re-selection.

Current best conditional proof skeleton for a negative resolution:
1. Produce a finite nonnegative seed b of length r with primitive capacity A=1+sum b>(r+1)^rho, rho=log_2(1+sqrt2).
2. Supply a nonnegative complete interpolation/PSD certificate for its intrinsic primitive energy; passing necessary cuts or all quadratics is insufficient.
3. Apply exact balanced primitive joins, preserving every physical step and every multiplier.
4. Convert to last-iterate objective certificates with the actual initial-distance term, using the terminal closure of fact1252b8823bb5a290 where applicable.
5. Prove the unbounded-horizon comparison to the exact B_n profile, including all constants in the amplification argument.
6. Obtain a fixed relative improvement and hence disprove the goal. No seed satisfying step1 is known. The amplification remains a draft conditional package, not a verified conclusion.

Central missing lemma for this particular architecture: there exist a finite r, b in [0,infinity)^r, and nonnegative coefficients on the complete ordered smooth-convex interpolation inequalities such that the intrinsic primitive energy has a full PSD identity and 1+sum b>(r+1)^rho. This existential claim may be false. It is not presented as a necessary condition for every possible negative answer, and its absence does not support the affirmative answer.

# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Signed-closed components:
- Huber lower witness and the fixed finite terminal-library obstruction under unchanged ConPD and stated prefix caps: fact0be8b54e0a33381c.

- Exact attained terminal completion and full terminal-square/prefix-PSD certificate for every nonnegative schedule, including zero and empty schedules: fact1252b8823bb5a290.

Every draft row above remains outside the accepted fact graph. Each exclusion retains the hypotheses of its actual construction class.

# 2. Interface contract table

### Exact terminal completion
**Input required.** The optimum and every ordered interpolation inequality on the genuinely queried prefix, together with outgoing terminal inequalities.
**Output claimed.** The maximal prefix interpolant realizes the exact attained terminal value; some full optimal certificate has a terminal square plus the complete prefix PSD remainder.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290, including zero and empty schedules.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** None for this exact component.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Repeating the deletion at queried samples changes subsequent GD positions and need not be valid.
**Status.** CLOSED.

### Primitive sign to projection geometry
**Input required.** A legal negative intrinsic-energy trajectory with its original nonnegative steps and all function values.
**Output claimed.** Drafts4ad60ca9f3ec3e2e and846c8ad5e763dfb7 give finite projection/simplex witnesses, retaining all normal inequalities and the negative sign. For F_C(x)=(||x||^2-dist(x,C)^2)/2, the intrinsic energy becomes ||x_0||^2-||x_n+S P_C(x_n)||^2.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 supplies interpolation background; the projection reductions themselves are drafts.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Complete reduction acceptance, and a quantitative relation between defect and the true closest-minimizer distance along growing horizons.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A translated-triangle example keeps negative raw energy while its normalized objective tends to zero.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Correlated projection walk to a capacity or objective inequality
**Input required.** One real Gram realization satisfying every projection normal inequality against every vertex and zero, with the actual chronological GD recursion.
**Output claimed.** C29 rules out a reduction to every quadratic and single cut; C30 gives a nonlinear rejection of that particular two-copy ruler. The actual constant-scale diagnostic is now the near-critical family in Bridge2.
**Available facts.** 0e7e78056dc6258c identifies the orthogonal limitation; 1fca3e5dd555cb4a and b98bb807d514e80d provide baseline comparators.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** A correlated family with target-relevant payoff and eventual arbitrary-schedule coverage.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A positive Gram matrix, code identity, probability distribution or local cocoercivity alone does not imply full interpolation.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Chronological nonnormal prefixes to a final-step lower witness
**Input required.** The same actual positions, gradients, legal values, real PSD geometry and every prefix cycle, with a terminal test vector satisfying all anchors.
**Output claimed.** Draft normal-mixture and real-Jordan obstructions narrow the remaining structured ansatz. The later compact-parabolic elliptic result is also scoped; general complex frequencies and more general nonnormal correlations remain outside these exclusions.
**Available facts.** dfde2fa326c908bf and1252b8823bb5a290.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** An explicit unbounded-horizon legal family beating the old channels at the required scale, or a complete obstruction for the next specified ansatz.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Individual-mode legality is not necessary for joint legality; conversely an arbitrary kernel is only an SDP reparameterization.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Signed terminal credit to a useful upper certificate
**Input required.** For a tail b, E_theta=V_b-theta sum_i b_i Q_(i,m), 0<=theta<1, with a complete nonnegative interpolation certificate and full PSD remainder. Saturated joins retain their actual lengths and all old query edges.
**Output claimed.** Draftedbbe4af2a2ad5c2 gives two-bridge composition and a final objective certificate. Its explicit calibration is strictly dominated and does not show cone enlargement.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 for final closure; 0be8b54e0a33381c for the limited unchanged-library obstruction.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** A tail for which the credit genuinely helps, sufficient physical capacity, and a valid infinite-horizon comparison; alternatively a general re-selection theorem.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Signed coordinates cannot replace nonnegative actual multipliers. The <=2-step, local-silver and fixed-middle2 exclusions do not prove general re-selection.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- **DANGEROUS:** "Primitive failure gives a uniform objective improvement." Its negative energy can survive a minimizer change while the initial-distance denominator grows. Draft20c6d21ee60a4cb0 provides a defect conversion with explicit instance-dependent losses, not the uniform profile theorem.
- **DANGEROUS:** "Every quadratic and every cut passing implies primitive validity." Draftc58273e20409955e shows those necessary tests admit arbitrarily excessive capacity. Nonlinear correlated geometry remains required.
- **DANGEROUS:** "The last-sample deletion can be repeated backwards." Fact1252b8823bb5a290 retains all queried-prefix constraints.
- **DANGEROUS:** "The four local silver witnesses globalize." Draft66067b85d1228261 passes all four at silver capacity but is defeated by an endpoint cut.
- **DANGEROUS:** "Failure of normal mixtures means all polynomial methods fail." Nonnormal correlations, higher-order clocks and more general structured kernels are outside that exclusion.
- **DANGEROUS:** "A fixed-prefix last-step minimax exchange optimizes every step." The fixed-prefix mixture result and the joint nonconvexity counterexample do not authorize earlier exchanges.

Do not discard old planes, old query heights or the true minimizer cost. Do not optimize an already dominated scalar transport without changing its mechanism. Do not replace the exact B_n profile by a decimal. Do not infer unrestricted upper bounds from failed lower-witness families. Do not promote a complete-looking draft to a fact. Do not equate finite-horizon capacity calibration with asymptotic minimax resolution.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge 1 -- strict signed-credit enlargement or general repair
**Target statement.** Either exhibit finite b>=0 and theta in[0,1) with a complete nonnegative full-PSD certificate for E_theta and a legal negative V_b witness, or prove an explicitly quantified re-selection implication from such E_theta certificates to ordinary primitive certificates for every b in a stated class.
**Prerequisites.** The precise Q and V conventions above and all actual multiplier signs. Validity and certifiability must not be identified without a proof for this cone.
**Existing facts to use.** 1252b8823bb5a290 for final objective conversion when applicable.
**Missing checks.** Actual cone separation or PSD-preserving repair; useful physical capacity if separation succeeds.
**Closure criterion.** A concrete separated object or complete repair theorem. The step from separation to an asymptotic improvement is additional. Independent of the lower-witness bridges.

### Bridge 2 -- attained genuinely varying geometry at the critical scale
**Target statement.** Let lambda=1+sqrt2,c_m=lambda(1+1/m),a_m=(1-1/m)/c_m,H_0=(),H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_m c_m^j,H_j),j<m, with parameters fixed per horizon n=2^m-1. Produce one globally legal convex1-smooth function and initial point for each sufficiently large m with quantitative objective payoff at the1/B_n scale, or a precisely scoped whole-construction obstruction. The actual function may depend on the full prescribed schedule. This is a diagnostic, not the arbitrary-schedule theorem.
**Prerequisites.** Every ordered interpolation pair or one explicit fixed function; true initial-distance normalization; the complete predetermined trajectory; free terminal completion if used. A scalar alternative requires one continuous nondecreasing1-Lipschitz derivative and its full areas. A joint spectral alternative requires one real Gram realization and all joint cycles, not individual-mode legality.
**Existing facts to use.** 1252b8823bb5a290 for exact completion,1fca3e5dd555cb4a andb98bb807d514e80d for baseline comparisons,0e7e78056dc6258c for the restricted orthogonal obstruction,dfde2fa326c908bf for the cycle interface.
**Missing checks.** An attained payoff from actual varying queried gradients, mixed-sign/longer-memory correlations, horizon-dependent scalar geometry, or a genuinely joint moment construction. The specified final-gradient-only, fresh-antialigned, tree-incidence and tested finite merge architectures have draft exclusions; these are not all nonlinear functions.
**Closure criterion.** Full legal data and a quantitative normalized payoff with every constant uniform in m, or a whole-ansatz obstruction identifying a different remaining mechanism. An old fixed-ratio estimate with deteriorating constants is insufficient. This bridge is independent of the upper-certificate route; successful data can support the all-final-step question in Bridge3.
### Bridge 3 -- objective payoff in the near-critical regime
**Target statement.** Let lambda=1+sqrt2, c_m=lambda(1+1/m), a_m=(1-1/m)/c_m, H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_m c_m^j,H_j) for j<m, and p_m be H_m without its last entry. Determine a rigorous lower bound for inf_(b>=0)W_(2^m-1)(p_m,b) relative to the exact B_(2^m-1), accounting for the full quadratic spectrum and all accepted baselines first.
**Prerequisites.** Parameters are fixed over each whole horizon; every adversary is globally legal and its true initial-distance denominator is used.
**Existing facts to use.** 1252b8823bb5a290, b98bb807d514e80d and1fca3e5dd555cb4a.
**Missing checks.** A constant-scale payoff or a precise demonstrated obstruction. No target ratio is assumed.
**Closure criterion.** A quantitative result for this actual family. Arbitrary-schedule/all-horizon coverage remains separate. Independent of Bridges1 and2.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.** Exact terminal completion is available, but neither a uniform sharp lower witness nor an asymptotically improving full-class upper certificate has been obtained.

The fixed goal is unchanged: for predetermined nonnegative GD steps, with
W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_*)/(L||x_0-x_*||^2), decide whether W_n^*/U_n^ZJ tends to one. The affirmative statement requires every epsilon>0 to admit N_epsilon independent of h such that W_n(h)>=(1-epsilon)U_n^ZJ for every n>=N_epsilon and every h in [0,infinity)^n. A negative resolution requires a fixed delta>0 and certified schedules on unbounded horizons with W_n(h)<=(1-delta)U_n^ZJ.

For the exact comparator, P_0=B_0=1,
F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2,
P_n=max_(k+l=n-1)F(P_k,P_l), B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(P_k,B_l), and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n. No decimal constant or assumed normalized limit replaces this sequence.
