**Output claimed.** The maximal prefix interpolant realizes the exact attained terminal value; some full optimal certificate has a terminal square plus the complete prefix PSD remainder.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290, including zero and empty schedules.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** None for this exact component.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Repeating the deletion at queried samples changes subsequent GD positions and need not be valid.
**Status.** CLOSED.

### Primitive sign to projection geometry
**Input required.** A legal negative intrinsic-energy trajectory with its original nonnegative steps and all function values.
**Output claimed.** Drafts4ad60ca9f3ec3e2e and846c8ad5e763dfb7 give finite projection/simplex witnesses, retaining all normal inequalities and the negative sign. For F_C(x)=(||x||^2-dist(x,C)^2)/2, the intrinsic energy becomes ||x_0||^2-||x_n+S P_C(x_n)||^2.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 supplies interpolation background; the projection reductions themselves are drafts.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Complete reduction acceptance, and a quantitative relation between defect and the true closest-minimizer distance along growing horizons.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A translated-triangle example keeps negative raw energy while its normalized objective tends to zero.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Correlated projection walk to a capacity or objective inequality
**Input required.** One real Gram realization satisfying every projection normal inequality against every vertex and zero, with the actual chronological GD recursion.
**Output claimed.** C29 rules out a reduction to every quadratic and single cut; C30 gives a nonlinear rejection of that particular two-copy ruler. The actual constant-scale diagnostic is now the near-critical family in Bridge2.
**Available facts.** 0e7e78056dc6258c identifies the orthogonal limitation; 1fca3e5dd555cb4a and b98bb807d514e80d provide baseline comparators.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** A correlated family with target-relevant payoff and eventual arbitrary-schedule coverage.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A positive Gram matrix, code identity, probability distribution or local cocoercivity alone does not imply full interpolation.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Chronological nonnormal prefixes to a final-step lower witness
**Input required.** The same actual positions, gradients, legal values, real PSD geometry and every prefix cycle, with a terminal test vector satisfying all anchors.
**Output claimed.** Draft normal-mixture and real-Jordan obstructions narrow the remaining structured ansatz. The later compact-parabolic elliptic result is also scoped; general complex frequencies and more general nonnormal correlations remain outside these exclusions.
**Available facts.** dfde2fa326c908bf and1252b8823bb5a290.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** An explicit unbounded-horizon legal family beating the old channels at the required scale, or a complete obstruction for the next specified ansatz.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Individual-mode legality is not necessary for joint legality; conversely an arbitrary kernel is only an SDP reparameterization.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Signed terminal credit to a useful upper certificate
**Input required.** For a tail b, E_theta=V_b-theta sum_i b_i Q_(i,m), 0<=theta<1, with a complete nonnegative interpolation certificate and full PSD remainder. Saturated joins retain their actual lengths and all old query edges.
**Output claimed.** Draftedbbe4af2a2ad5c2 gives two-bridge composition and a final objective certificate. Its explicit calibration is strictly dominated and does not show cone enlargement.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 for final closure; 0be8b54e0a33381c for the limited unchanged-library obstruction.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** A tail for which the credit genuinely helps, sufficient physical capacity, and a valid infinite-horizon comparison; alternatively a general re-selection theorem.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Signed coordinates cannot replace nonnegative actual multipliers. The <=2-step, local-silver and fixed-middle2 exclusions do not prove general re-selection.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- **DANGEROUS:** "Primitive failure gives a uniform objective improvement." Its negative energy can survive a minimizer change while the initial-distance denominator grows. Draft20c6d21ee60a4cb0 provides a defect conversion with explicit instance-dependent losses, not the uniform profile theorem.
- **DANGEROUS:** "Every quadratic and every cut passing implies primitive validity." Draftc58273e20409955e shows those necessary tests admit arbitrarily excessive capacity. Nonlinear correlated geometry remains required.
- **DANGEROUS:** "The last-sample deletion can be repeated backwards." Fact1252b8823bb5a290 retains all queried-prefix constraints.
- **DANGEROUS:** "The four local silver witnesses globalize." Draft66067b85d1228261 passes all four at silver capacity but is defeated by an endpoint cut.
- **DANGEROUS:** "Failure of normal mixtures means all polynomial methods fail." Nonnormal correlations, higher-order clocks and more general structured kernels are outside that exclusion.
- **DANGEROUS:** "A fixed-prefix last-step minimax exchange optimizes every step." The fixed-prefix mixture result and the joint nonconvexity counterexample do not authorize earlier exchanges.

Do not discard old planes, old query heights or the true minimizer cost. Do not optimize an already dominated scalar transport without changing its mechanism. Do not replace the exact B_n profile by a decimal. Do not infer unrestricted upper bounds from failed lower-witness families. Do not promote a complete-looking draft to a fact. Do not equate finite-horizon capacity calibration with asymptotic minimax resolution.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge 1 -- a genuinely full-objective recursive certificate
**Target statement.** For H_0=(),H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_m c_m^j,H_j),j<m, with c_m=(1+sqrt2)(1+1/m),a_m=(1-1/m)/c_m, construct an explicit full-objective q_m/multiplier/remainder family at n=2^m-1, or a concrete obstruction to a specified proposed family. A fixed-constant deformation must be fully stated and its physical nonnegativity proved. The target comparison is limsup q_m B_n<1, not a primitive-capacity proxy.
**Prerequisites.** All actual interpolation coefficients nonnegative, all function values canceled, the actual initial-distance term, and PSD on the full formal space. The undeformed known benchmark and any finite patterns only calibrate the formula; they do not imply its extension.
**Existing facts to use.** 1252b8823bb5a290 supplies exact unqueried-terminal completion/normal form. The exact benchmark recurrences are retained from the problem and the baseline facts.
**Missing checks.** An actual coefficient family, its uniform full-matrix positivity and every asymptotic cost. The draft limsup B_n K_m<=2/e suggests that a proved q_m<=beta K_m with beta<e/2 would suffice, but that comparator and its exact assumptions must also be included. This sufficient inequality is not assumed necessary.
**Closure criterion.** A complete general-m identity and strict exact-profile comparison, or a concrete failed-ansatz witness identifying what remains. Generic SDP feasibility and a finite coefficient fit are insufficient. This is independent of the lower-witness constructions in Bridge2.
### Bridge 2 -- attained genuinely varying geometry at the critical scale
**Target statement.** Let lambda=1+sqrt2,c_m=lambda(1+1/m),a_m=(1-1/m)/c_m,H_0=(),H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_m c_m^j,H_j),j<m, with parameters fixed per horizon n=2^m-1. Produce one globally legal convex1-smooth function and initial point for each sufficiently large m with quantitative objective payoff at the1/B_n scale, or a precisely scoped whole-construction obstruction. The actual function may depend on the full prescribed schedule. This is a diagnostic, not the arbitrary-schedule theorem.
**Prerequisites.** Every ordered interpolation pair or one explicit fixed function; true initial-distance normalization; the complete predetermined trajectory; free terminal completion if used. A scalar alternative requires one continuous nondecreasing1-Lipschitz derivative and its full areas. A joint spectral alternative requires one real Gram realization and all joint cycles, not individual-mode legality.
**Existing facts to use.** 1252b8823bb5a290 for exact completion,1fca3e5dd555cb4a andb98bb807d514e80d for baseline comparisons,0e7e78056dc6258c for the restricted orthogonal obstruction,dfde2fa326c908bf for the cycle interface.
**Missing checks.** An attained payoff from actual varying queried gradients, mixed-sign/longer-memory correlations, horizon-dependent scalar geometry, or a genuinely joint moment construction. The specified final-gradient-only, fresh-antialigned, tree-incidence and tested finite merge architectures have draft exclusions; these are not all nonlinear functions.
**Closure criterion.** Full legal data and a quantitative normalized payoff with every constant uniform in m, or a whole-ansatz obstruction identifying a different remaining mechanism. An old fixed-ratio estimate with deteriorating constants is insufficient. This bridge is independent of the upper-certificate route; successful data can support the all-final-step question in Bridge3.
### Bridge 3 -- objective payoff in the near-critical regime
**Target statement.** Let lambda=1+sqrt2, c_m=lambda(1+1/m), a_m=(1-1/m)/c_m, H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_m c_m^j,H_j) for j<m, and p_m be H_m without its last entry. Determine a rigorous lower bound for inf_(b>=0)W_(2^m-1)(p_m,b) relative to the exact B_(2^m-1), accounting for the full quadratic spectrum and all accepted baselines first.
**Prerequisites.** Parameters are fixed over each whole horizon; every adversary is globally legal and its true initial-distance denominator is used.
**Existing facts to use.** 1252b8823bb5a290, b98bb807d514e80d and1fca3e5dd555cb4a.
**Missing checks.** A constant-scale payoff or a precise demonstrated obstruction. No target ratio is assumed.
**Closure criterion.** A quantitative result for this actual family. Arbitrary-schedule/all-horizon coverage remains separate. Independent of Bridges1 and2.

## 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.** The exact all-schedule profile converse and a full-class
asymptotically improved schedule are both missing.

independent of the finite nonnegative schedule h such that every n>=N_epsilon
has W_n(h)>=(1-epsilon)/B_n, or there must be a rigorous fixed relative
improvement along unbounded horizons. Here W_n(h)=sup 2(f_n-f_star)/
(L||x0-x_star||^2), over all finite-dimensional convex L-smooth functions
with a minimizer, and B_n is the exact recurrence below. Keep W=2R.

constructions, and exact unqueried-terminal completion/re-selection

is a full objective certificate on a precisely defined critical dyadic word,
followed by a new scalar comparison. The main blocker is actual nonnegative
full-array feasibility and PSD at growing depths, not scalar asymptotics.
The affirmative architecture still lacks a lossless quantitative all-schedule
construction or necessary dual inequality.

The new evidence establishes method failure in several stated classes, not
proposition failure. Neither a finite Huber match nor a full-metric exclusion
within a fixed temporal span gives the unrestricted conclusion. SUBSTANTIAL

completion.

Current best proof skeleton:
1. Fix lambda=1+sqrt2 and the exact P/B recurrences below.
2. At depth m fix c_m=lambda(1+1/m), a_m=(1-1/m)/c_m for the whole word.

Central missing lemma: for the words in steps 2-3, there exist m0 and a
constant C<e/lambda such that every m>=m0 has q_m<=C/M_m and nonnegative
coefficients Lambda_ij whose smooth-convex interpolation combination cancels
all values in q_m||x0||^2-2f_n+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij and leaves a PSD quadratic
form on the entire independent formal space. Sign convention for Q must be

or a replacement of the original affirmative goal. The exact finite n=7

## 2. Interface contract table

### Exact benchmark comparison
**Input required.** P0=B0=1,
F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2,
P_n=max_(k+l=n-1)F(P_k,P_l), B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(P_k,B_l).
**Output claimed.** C54 proves G(x,lambda y)<=lambda F(x,y), hence
B_n<=lambda P_n, P_n<=(n+1)^rho, rho=log_2 lambda, with equality for P on
dyadic horizons. The actual word has M_m/lambda^m->e.
**Available facts.** b98bb807d514e80d supplies the benchmark definitions.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** The new comparison proof is
still a draft; it gives no objective upper bound.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Scalar capacity is mistaken for an all-function
performance guarantee.
**Status.** SUBSTANTIAL.

### Full upper array and original-space PSD
**Input required.** A full coefficient array, nonnegative on every actual
interpolation pair, exact value flows, and the true x0-distance coefficient.
**Output claimed.** Such an array implies W_n(h)<=q by interpolation.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 retains every queried-prefix condition
and allows exact unqueried-terminal completion/re-selection.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** No growing-depth array at the
required q is known. C51 047c257795dc59ba gives an exact finite H_3 draft and
lossless coordinates only at the Huber value. C55 retains a genuinely new
third boundary coordinate but has no feasible all-depth invariant.

subspace is confused with PSD on the entire space. Huber-only kernel
constraints exclude potentially useful q>Huber certificates.

### Growing legal temporal span
**Input required.** Actual joint values and vectors obeying every smooth-
convex interpolation pair and the true GD recursion/normalization.
**Output claimed.** A legal transcript and completion yield a lower witness.
**Available facts.** dfde2fa326c908bf and 1252b8823bb5a290 provide the scoped
realization and completion interfaces.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** No new structured growing
temporal span with exact profile-scale payoff has been supplied. Fixed-span
full-metric closure and arbitrary-span equivalence leave a genuine gap.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Spectral radius, PSD of a chosen metric or
pairwise local tests replace full chronology and cycle legality.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Quantitative queried-cut lower bridge
**Input required.** Arbitrary nonnegative schedules and actual full objective
certificates, including remote interpolation flows and degenerate kernels.
**Output claimed.** A quantitative necessary cut cost would constrain possible
q, if its state can be iterated without losing the exact profile constant.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 supplies the full reduced formulation;
the selected lower constructions supply calibrations.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Draft queried incidence is
only qualitative. No scalar capacity, lossless separator, or all-cut iteration
has been proved for arbitrary arrays.
**Failure mode if ignored.** An unproved separator of minimax strength is
labeled a routine induction, or a bounded-step result is applied to long steps.

## 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "The critical word is Huber-tight because H_3 is." Status: DANGEROUS.
  The finite certificate does not construct all depths. Larger q remains
  sufficient and may need a different optimum-edge and kernel structure.
- "A balanced pair fixes the old bad vector, therefore the certificate is fixed."
  Status: FALSE AS STATED. C55's pair increment is indefinite on the original
  space; the complete Schur/range conditions remain necessary.
- "Small modes remain harmless under every metric." Status: FALSE AS STATED.
  c5a096d73740bf81 is an actual legal counterexample. With sufficiently many
  modes, an unrestricted metric can encode the original problem.
- "Exact terminal elimination can be repeated at positive queried vertices."
  Status: FALSE AS STATED. Positive-query incidence and the fixed GD update
  prevent that deletion; the accepted terminal fact explicitly retains them.
- "An endpoint-fan no-go refutes the schedule." Status: DANGEROUS.
  The negative formal template itself violates a queried pair. It refutes
  the stated certificate array, not the existence of all arrays or a function.

Strategies not to pursue:
- Do not reopen the closed two-mode span by another covariance amplitude.
- Do not rename an unrestricted Gram formulation as a new finite-field method.
- Do not infer full PSD from the returned numerical primal span.
- Do not promote C54 or C51 to fact status without actual acceptance.
- Do not trade an unspecified constant or exponent-only theorem for the exact B_n target.
- Do not discard zero steps, optimum edges, or singular range conditions.

## 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge 1 — C55 all-depth three-boundary closure
**Target statement.** For every sufficiently large m, the attained seed and
six-variable join in draft 1f81b116bd1128ab admit predetermined parameters at

C<e/lambda, without the endpoint-fan support restrictions already refuted.

### Bridge 3 — Quantitative lower structure
**Target statement.** Supply either an explicit growing structured legal

## 2. Interface contract table

### Full numerical geometry to exact primal/certificate
**Input required.** Complete original-space primal/dual arrays, exact step
formula, every interpolation pair and true initial normalization.
**Output claimed.** The C58 finite computations supply only numerical data.
For actual H3/H4 their reported values are near Huber. For H5, the reported
objective is0.00999083452192234 versus K_Hub0.0059193034396359,
with M*payoff0.8489151980094273. For H6 it is0.021804003331820192,
with M*payoff4.834126046845032 and ratio to Huber9.646448090358243.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 supplies the exact target formulation.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** H3/H4/H6 are flagged
optimal_inaccurate; all outputs retain nonzero residuals. H5/H6 full-original
dual minima are approximately-2.63e-6 and-8.30e-5, respectively. No exact
feasible primal or dual has been reconstructed from these new cases.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Numerical feasibility or a small dual gap is
mistaken for an exact certificate; finite behavior is extrapolated to infinity.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Whole-array continuation from an actual Silver certificate
**Input required.** A genuinely valid all-depth full baseline, all actual
interpolation coefficients and its complete remainder, with correct W scale.
**Output claimed.** A uniformly controlled feasible deformation could give
the missing asymptotic upper certificate.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290; no accepted deformation theorem.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** One-sided coefficient signs,
full nullspace/mixed-block range conditions, finite deformation bounds and
uniformity in m. C51's Huber-specific constraints are not imposed at larger q.
**Failure mode if ignored.** One-step calibration, a fixed-depth IFT or a
kernel derivative substitutes for the actual finite all-depth path.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Proper increment cone and legal correlated witness
**Input required.** One common nonnegative flow array tested on every
three-block gradient restriction, or its stated dual primal Gram cone.
**Output claimed.** Draft e01c11b179127b30 gives equality and both attainments
between that common-array value and a legal full-interpolation payoff with
pair-supported PSD gradient-increment blocks, for every h>=0 including zero
steps. It remains a proper cone.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 and0e7e78056dc6258c are its accepted inputs.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** The new characterization is a
draft; its value at1/M_m and its uniform exact-B_n comparison are unknown.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A matrix-only non-PSD example is called an actual
coefficient array; orthogonal gradients or independent per-cut arrays are
mistaken for the required correlated common object.
**Status.** SUBSTANTIAL.

### Joint positive-height double-zero quotient
**Input required.** One shared function-value array, all cross-mode inner
products, every earlier/earlier and suffix pair, actual optimum and distance.
**Output claimed.** The explicit quotient gives a restricted growing
trajectory family and exact interpolation tests.
**Available facts.** 1252b8823bb5a290 and b98bb807d514e80d.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** A global height budget or a
useful legal positive-height realization. The isolated-level charge cannot
simply be added across jointly correlated modes.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Zero-height results or independently assigned
modal values are extended to the joint problem without justification.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

## 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "H3/H4 are Huber-like, so the family is Huber-tight." Status: DANGEROUS.
  The new finite numerical data already point to a different regime. Even
  an exact finite list would not settle an asymptotic statement.
- "H6 numerically violates1/M, so the entire asymptotic candidate is disproved."
  Status: DANGEROUS. This needs exact legality and an unbounded-horizon argument;
  the target may begin only at sufficiently large horizons.
- "The relaxed cut model is attained, therefore its exact profile is known."
  Status: FALSE AS STATED. The hard quantitative value has not been established.
- "An auxiliary repair works, so its normalized payoff is strong." Status:
  DANGEROUS. C56/C57 expose initial-distance and ordering costs on their classes.
- "Dense tensor support removes every restriction." Status: FALSE AS STATED.
  The normalized mixture still ties first and terminal coefficients too tightly.

Do not pursue another monotone/acute variant inside C57; do not force zero
optimum-source weights at q>Huber; do not delete remote queried pairs; do not
repair numerical eigenvalues and relabel raw data as exact; do not use a
restricted-schedule lower witness as the all-h theorem; do not turn a
theorem-equivalent singleton refinement into a purported bridge.

## 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge 1 — An exact scalable non-Huber witness
**Target statement.** Give an explicit finite-dimensional legal trajectory
and one smooth convex function for each horizon of a concrete unbounded
family, with a quantitatively useful payoff against exact B_n or the tested
critical upper candidate. The evidence suggests signed/correlated gradients;
no particular limiting rank or sign pattern is assumed.
**Prerequisites.** All ordered pairs, a true minimizer, initial distance,
predetermined steps and a fully specified common value array.
**Existing facts to use.** 1252b8823bb5a290 supplies realization/completion.
**Missing checks.** Exact reconstruction of a generating pattern and uniform
payoff. A finite reconstructed witness is only a structural starting point.
**Closure criterion.** A full exact family and proved constants; to resolve
the original affirmative target it must further cover EVERY h and horizon.
Independent of Bridge2.

### Bridge 2 — Uniform full-array continuation
**Target statement.** Construct nonnegative complete arrays on an explicit
deformed schedule with q_m B_(n_m)<=1-delta eventually, for one fixed delta>0.
**Prerequisites.** A valid all-depth baseline, full PSD and true objective scale.
**Existing facts to use.** 1252b8823bb5a290 and the exact benchmark definitions;
include the unverified C54 scalar proof if that comparison is used.
**Missing checks.** Actual feasible coefficient path and horizon-uniform
control, including singular and newly activated edges.
**Closure criterion.** An explicit complete certificate family, not a tangent
direction or finite solver output. Independent alternative to Bridge1.

### Bridge 3 — Quantitative correlated increment/height policy
**Target statement.** On the proper increment cone or joint positive-height
quotient, supply one explicit correlated generating policy, complete legality
and exact target-scale payoff, or a scoped exact cost obstruction showing
why that policy cannot deliver it.
**Prerequisites.** One joint object rather than independent pair/modal solutions.
**Existing facts to use.** 1252b8823bb5a290, b98bb807d514e80d and the scoped
orthogonal obstruction0e7e78056dc6258c as a calibration warning.
**Missing checks.** The quantitative policy and its constant-preserving
iteration; a generic covariance parameterization is not sufficient.
**Closure criterion.** A genuine explicit construction or definite obstruction
that changes the next mechanism. The original all-h comparison stays open
until separately proved. This can support Bridge1 in parallel with Bridge2.

## 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.** The exact all-horizon/all-schedule profile comparison remains
open. A finite diagnostic is now accepted, while the newer universal
projection/simplex descriptions are reductions without a sharp minimax bound.

Fixed goal: W_n^*/U_n^ZJ ->1, or a rigorous asymptotic disproof, where
W_n(h)=sup 2(f_n-f_star)/(L||x0-x_star||^2), h is predetermined and
nonnegative, and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n. Every dimension is finite but may grow.
An affirmative proof needs one N_epsilon independent of h for ALL n>=N_epsilon;
a negative proof needs a fixed relative gap along unbounded horizons. W=2R.
P0=B0=1, F(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2,
P_n=max_(k+l=n-1)F(P_k,P_l), B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(P_k,B_l).

Seven scoped facts are accepted, including the genuine finite H6 adversary
e9a55f57461f253c. It does not decide eventual critical-word behavior or the
original minimax ratio. The projection-simplex and two-state constructions
are concrete potential languages for a proof, but their necessary quantitative
estimates remain missing. Current failures are method failure in named
families, not proposition failure. PARTIAL/SUBSTANTIAL do not mean almost solved.

Current best proof skeleton, conditional on a real quantitative mechanism:
1. Retain the exact benchmark recurrences and original normalized function class.
2. Specify an actual legal function/trajectory for each needed h, or a full nonnegative objective array for an improving schedule family.
3. Retain every queried pair, zero-step case, finite dimension and closest-distance normalization.

W_n(h)>=(1-epsilon)/B_n for every h>=0 and all sufficiently large n with an
h-independent threshold, or an explicit certificate family must deliver
q_(n_j) B_(n_j)<=1-delta for a fixed delta>0. As a bare statement this is
minimax-strength and remains a blocked interface; rewriting it as a Gram
game or a projection problem does not constitute progress on the bound.

# Self-authored strategy after the new shared constructions

Consult remains off. This is main-agent strategy based on the concrete shared state, not an external-model reply or a correctness judgment. The exact PROBLEM.md target, all nonnegative schedules, full horizon-dependent benchmark, Astra/max requirement, current four running workers and cumulative round cap 5 remain unchanged. Elaboration feb116fbcd401d6a records the mathematical synthesis. All five allocated verifier submissions are used; no additional routine verification is allocated here. A complete target candidate still requires independent attack followed by the selected complete verification package.

The latest useful changes are the genuinely signed-history positive-value relay (8cce883b9f93f556), the exact conditional suffix deformation (8c998d29efd3da99), the fixed-function interval reachability model (7e14f9c47de04c17), and the whole-tail residual calibration failure (4be2737d7e8764a6). These are unverified research objects. Their precise unmatched hypotheses now drive the next work; do not continue producing isolated examples while leaving the same interface untouched.

max: move from periodic legal examples to one exact nonstationary cross-block composition with positive proximal values. The required object is a data-level joining rule, including all old/new plane inequalities and its normalized payoff loss. The previous minimum-reset activation obstruction remains a hostile test. A hypothesis saying that all those inequalities are feasible at the target scale is not a joining theorem. Where useful, interpret correlations probabilistically or use a real Gram construction, but attach all function values and minimizer normalization to the same data. Keep this lane distinct from scalar interval dynamics.

max2: the triangular suffix deformation is actual new algebra, but triangularity cannot be assumed at a global minimizer. First attack the smallest new unrestricted case: star-outgoing flow at n-2 with two steps remaining, allowing every forward finite-index multiplier. Seek an actual simultaneous schedule/flow change with full PSD preservation or controlled PSD gain. Interval/cycle coordinates may organize finite-edge changes, provided aggregate multipliers stay nonnegative. If impossible, give a concrete complete-certificate obstruction and say exactly which extension is false. Do not present another triangular special case or bare stationarity identity as removing this gap. Retain the longer-term exact-profile mass-cap gap even if Huber tightness is obtained.

max3: use the exact interval-image formulation to build or exclude a genuinely multi-knot fixed scalar gradient. The payoff must include the same fixed gradient's complete terminal area. Focus on the recursive hostile family and at least one non-homogeneous crossing pattern that the earlier outward/cap/two-curvature channels miss. An exact constructive rule or a scoped analytic obstruction changes the next action; an equivalent optimization problem or another small finite scan alone does not. Timewise independent choices of gradient, knot position or slope are not legal. Keep the general scalar class separate from symmetric Huber and from arbitrary multidimensional functions.

max4: retain both objective and primitive dual endpoint residuals in the nonlocal tail law, with the bridge gradient and tail initial gradient kept distinct. Supply the actual interpolation-coefficient identity and full boundary matrix, then test whether it can reproduce the known recursive benchmark without a fixed loss before searching for an improvement. The tested aggregate-only fixed-residual law has a quantitative failure and should not be repeatedly retuned. Above-power primitive-seed amplification is now a conditional tool, not a found seed. The 600-evaluation numerical screen is exhausted and stays closed; this is an analytic assignment and does not extend numerical or verifier allocations.

All lanes must publish a concrete new interface, exact counterexample, or precise mechanism-specific obstruction, with a stated next action. Mark a theorem-strength unmatched condition as open rather than technical. Small exact calibration is discovery work, not a proof of the asymptotic goal. Neither the recursive phase literature nor the older exponent reports supply unrestricted schedule optimality. Refresh shared guidance at a mechanism switch; do not overwrite another worker's research artifacts.

  the virtual terminal-norm monotonicity shortcut is false.
-FINAL C10305e5148634a4a26f closes the full declared nonradial orthogonal
  and specific safe-angle laws at JOINT-DRAFT level. max3's current

-Dormant ff8f8f21b222a81b/7bc886cf037bdcd4/5f37096a5102c611 leave the
  chronological kernel OPEN, not refuted. The proposed distinct search
  retains JOINT conditional covariance in a full block or martingale
